disassemble)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# DERIVED\\n# =============================================================================\\nCAM_R_IN = CAM_ID / 2.0   # 45.35mm\\nCAM_R_OUT= CAM_OD / 2.0   # 70.0mm\\nSHAFT_R  = SHAFT_D / 2.0\\nBOLT_R   =      # 62.35mm \\u2014 end of stitch zone\\nR4 = R3 + LOWER_Z           # 67.35mm \\u2014 end of lower zone\\n\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\nprint(\\\"CSM V3 CAM RING \\u2014 R3+ FINAL\\\")\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\nprint(f\\\"OD={CAM_OD}  ID={CAM_ID}  H={CAM_H}  Wall={CAM_WALL:.2f}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"Radial gap to cylinder: {RAD_GAP:.3f}mm  \\u2705 (target 0.35mm)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"Reserve zone: {RESERVE_Z:.2f}mm\\\")\\nprint()\\nprint(\\\"Zone radii:\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Bore:    R={CAM_R_IN:.2f}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Raise\\u2192:  R={R1:.2f}mm  (zone Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_R + 2, CAM_H + 2)\\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\\ncam = cam.cut(shft)\\n\\n# --- Needle butt track (circumferential groove on bore face) ---\\nt_out = holes (4\\u00d7 M5, PCD 130mm, with countersinks) ---\\nfor i in range(BOLT_N):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 90.0 + 45.0)\\n    bx, by = BOLT_R * math.cos(ang), BOLT_R * math.sin(ang)\\n    bh = cs.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, CAM_H - 5))\\n    cam = cam.cut(cs)\\n\\n# --- Alignment pin holes (3\\u00d7 \\u00d84mm, depth 10mm, H7/g6 slip) ---\\n# H7/g6: hole = +0.018/+0.000mm over nominal key slot (sliding fit \\u2014 NOT press) ---\\n# Key slot = key + clearance, sliding so cam ring can be removed\\nks = Part.makeBox(KEY_W + 0.2, KEY_DEPTH + 0.1, KEY_H *  Layer:       0.15mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Perimeters:  5 (inner bore faces must be dense)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Top/Bottom:  6 solid layers\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Infill:      50% \\u2705 LOCKED\\nSLOT_RADIAL = 3.0       # mm \\u2014 radial penetration into 12mm wall\\n\\n# Needle entry geometry \\u2014 R3+ NEW\\nENTRY_CHAMFER  = 0.4    # mm \\u2014 lead-in chamfer width on top =============================================================================\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\nCutting {NEEDLE_N} slots with chamfered entry...\\\")\\n\\nfor i in range(NEEDLE_N):\\n    ang = i * \\u26a0\\ufe0f TEMPORARY = 0.85mm (NEEDLE_T 0.75mm + 0.10mm clearance)\\n\\nNeedle entry:\\n  Lead-in chamfer: 0.4mm on top edge of each slot\\n  Compound radius: 0.5mm at chamfer-to-slot 6 solid layers\\nInfill global: 30\\u201340% Gyroid\\nInfill slot zone: 100% Rectilinear \\u2014 use MODIFIER MESH:\\n  \\u2192 Create annular cylinder modifier: OD=94mm, ID=84mm, H=55mm\\n  \\u2192     "text": " Understood on all points. No documentation until we finalize these three design decisions. Here are my engineering opinions, direct and without padding.\n\n---\n\n## Question 1: Cassette base \u2014 solid or pocketed?\n\n**Answer: Pocketed, but conservatively.**\n\nThe cassette base rotates at machine speed carrying the full cassette weight (~300-400g). Reducing rotating mass directly reduces motor load, vibration, and bearing wear. However, pockets must preserve:\n\n- Full material around all 4 bolt holes (\u22658mm wall around each insert)\n- Full material around all 3 pin holes (\u22656mm wall around each pin)\n- Full material at boss perimeter (boss must not flex under cassette load)\n\nRecommended pocket layout: 6 symmetrical lightening pockets, \u00d820mm each, at R=55mm, equally spaced 60\u00b0 apart \u2014 avoiding pin and bolt positions. This removes ~25-30% of mass while keeping all load paths intact. The OD flange (outer 15mm ring) stays solid for rigidity.\n\n---\n\n## Question 2: Belt pulley \u2014 on cylinder directly or separate hub?\n\n**Answer: Separate hub, mounted below cassette base on the shaft.**\n\nMounting the pulley directly on the cylinder creates three problems. First, belt tension (~15-25N) acts tangentially on the cylinder wall, introducing a bending moment on the cylinder-to-shaft interface \u2014 exactly where your slots already reduce wall stiffness. Second, you lose the ability to swap cylinders (60\u219272\u219284N) without removing and reconfiguring the belt. Third, the pulley OD (~40mm for 60T GT2) sits very close to the cam ring OD, creating clearance problems.\n\nCorrect architecture:\n```\nSHAFT (12mm)\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 Cassette (top) \u2014 slides on/off for gauge swaps\n  \u2502     \u2514\u2500\u2500 cylinder + cam + sinker + retainer\n  \u2502\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 Drive hub (bottom, fixed to shaft)\n        \u2514\u2500\u2500 60T GT2 pulley\n        \u2514\u2500\u2500 Shaft collar (locks hub axially)\n        \u2514\u2500\u2500 Encoder disc (future)\n```\n\nThe drive hub is a separate 3D printed + brass insert part, fixed to the shaft below the cassette base with a set screw and keyway. Belt tension loads go directly into the shaft, not the cassette. Cassette removal for gauge swaps takes 4 bolts \u2014 pulley stays installed.\n\n---\n\n## Question 3: Bearing housing \u2014 integrated into frame or printed blocks?\n\n**Answer: Printed blocks, but with aluminum plate backing.**\n\nFully integrated (machined into frame) is ideal but requires CNC you don't have. Fully printed blocks without backing will loosen over time \u2014 bearing preload gradually compresses the PETG.\n\nThe right compromise for your hybrid frame:\n\n```\n6mm aluminum plate (top AND bottom)\n    \u2193 bolted to 2020 extrusion\nPrinted bearing block (H7 bore, press-fit bearing)\n    \u2193 M5 bolted through aluminum into extrusion T-nuts\n2020 extrusion (structural backbone)\n```\n\nThe aluminum plate does two things: distributes bearing preload force over a large area (preventing PETG creep), and gives you a rigid datum surface for shaft alignment. The printed block gives you the precision bore geometry the aluminum plate can't provide economically.\n\nCritical: the top and bottom bearing plates must be machined flat or shimmed flat to each other \u2014 shaft verticality depends on both plates being parallel within \u00b10.1mm. Check with a dial indicator after assembly.\n\n---\n\n## Now: Cam Profile Geometry\n\nWith those three decisions clarified, the cam profile is the right next step. To define it correctly, one question first:\n\n**What is your target knitting speed in RPM?**\n\nThis matters because cam ramp angles are a function of speed. A raise cam that works smoothly at 60 RPM will cause butt chatter and breakage at 200 RPM if the angle is too steep. The angle must be shallow enough that the needle accelerates gradually rather than impacting the cam face.\n\nGive me your target operating speed and I'll define all four cam zones with correct geometry.",

Retrofitting multi-feed later would mean redesigning the entire cam ring.\n\n---\n\n## Multi-feed cam: my engineering opinion\n\nFor a single-cylinder machine targeting patterned fashion socks, **2 feeds is the realistic and correct target**. Here's why not 1, and why not 4:\n\n**1 feed** limits you to plain stockinette or very basic stripes. For patterned fashion socks you need at minimum 2 feeds \u2014 one for ground yarn, one for pattern/plating.\n\n**4 feeds** (Lonati-style) requires synchronized multi-motor control, precise electronic yarn finger timing, and cam geometry that's genuinely difficult to prototype in PETG. It's Phase 5+ territory.\n\n**2 feeds** gives you: plating, 2-color patterns, elastic laid-in, and tuck stitches \u2014 everything needed for attractive fashion socks \u2014 with a cam ring that's still manufacturable on your printer.\n\n---\n\n## What multi-feed means for the cam ring geometry\n\nEach feed point requires its own **raise + hold + stitch + lower** cam sequence, positioned around the 360\u00b0 ring. With 2 feeds they are placed 180\u00b0 apart:\n\n```\nFEED 1 cam sequence:  0\u00b0 \u2192 180\u00b0\nFEED 2 cam sequence:  180\u00b0 \u2192 360\u00b0\n\nFull cam map (2-feed):\n\n  0\u00b0\u201335\u00b0    \u2192 RAISE 1\n  35\u00b0\u201360\u00b0   \u2192 HOLD 1\n  60\u00b0\u2013120\u00b0  \u2192 STITCH DROP 1\n  120\u00b0\u2013170\u00b0 \u2192 LOWER 1\n  170\u00b0\u2013180\u00b0 \u2192 TRANSITION / IDLE\n\n  180\u00b0\u2013215\u00b0 \u2192 RAISE 2\n  215\u00b0\u2013240\u00b0 \u2192 HOLD 2\n  240\u00b0\u2013300\u00b0 \u2192 STITCH DROP 2\n  300\u00b0\u2013350\u00b0 \u2192 LOWER 2\n  350\u00b0\u2013360\u00b0 \u2192 TRANSITION back to 0\u00b0\n```\n\nEach needle passes through both feed sequences every revolution \u2014 so you get **2 loops formed per needle per revolution**, doubling productivity vs single feed.\n\n---\n\n## Impact on cam ring dimensions\n\nThe good news: your cam ring OD=140mm, ID=90.7mm, H=25mm is **already sufficient for 2-feed**. The 24.7mm radial wall accommodates two independent cam track systems stacked axially (one in the upper half, one in the lower half of the 25mm height) or as two separate cam insert pockets at 180\u00b0 positions.\n\nThe two viable architectures:\n\n**Option A \u2014 Axial split (upper/lower half)**\n```\nFeed 1 cam track: Z = 5mm to 15mm (upper 10mm of cam H)\nFeed 2 cam track: Z = 10mm to 20mm (lower 10mm of cam H)\nOverlap zone:      Z = 10mm to 15mm (shared neutral)\n```\nSimpler to print. Both tracks are in the same ring. Requires precise axial positioning of yarn feeder height.\n\n**Option B \u2014 Two separate stitch cam inserts at 180\u00b0**\n```\nInsert 1: at 0\u00b0 position  (Feed 1 stitch cam)\nInsert 2: at 180\u00b0 position (Feed 2 stitch cam)\nBoth independently adjustable with set screws\n```\nMore flexible \u2014 you can tune each feed's loop size independently. This is closer to how Lonati does it. Slightly more complex to machine/print but your 25mm cam height accommodates both inserts without modification.\n\n---\n\n## My recommendation: Option B\n\nTwo independent stitch cam inserts at 180\u00b0 gives you:\n\n- Per-feed loop size adjustment (critical for plating where feed 1 and feed 2 yarns need different tensions)\n- Easier debugging (you can disable one insert to test single-feed first)\n- Direct upgrade path to 4-feed by adding 2 more inserts at 90\u00b0 and 270\u00b0\n\nThe cam ring H=25mm and OD=140mm don't need to change. The only addition is a second stitch cam insert pocket cut into the cam ring at the 180\u00b0 position, with its own 2\u00d7 M3 set screws.\n\n---\n\n## Three decisions needed before cam profile is finalized\n\n**1. Feed count:** Confirm 2-feed (I'm recommending yes).\n\n**2. Cam track arrangement:** Option A (axial split) or Option B (two inserts at 180\u00b0) \u2014 I recommend Option B.\n\n**3. Stitch depth per feed:** Do you want both feeds at the same depth (same loop size, good for plain plating), or independent adjustment (more complex patterns, different yarn weights per feed)? Option B gives you independent; Option A shares one stitch cam.\n\nAnswer these three and I'll finalize the complete cam geometry \u2014 all four zones, both feeds, correct angular spans, radial depths, and surface profile \u2014 ready for FreeCAD.",

    "type": "text",
    "text": " Good final question before we commit to the cam ring geometry. Here's my direct engineering answer.\n\n---\n\n## Straight slot inserts \u2014 and here's why\n\n**Dovetail inserts** are better in metal. In PETG on a FDM printer, they're a liability:\n\n- The dovetail undercut requires either printing at an angle (weakens the feature) or support material inside a precision slot (leaves rough surfaces exactly where you need smooth ones)\n- FDM layer lines running perpendicular to the dovetail face create a natural delamination plane under radial load\n- Tolerance stack on a dovetail profile printed at 0.15mm layers is \u00b10.2\u20130.3mm per face \u2014 the dovetail provides no meaningful improvement over a straight slot at that tolerance\n\n**Straight slot inserts** with a two-point constraint system give you equivalent precision through a different mechanism \u2014 the slot controls radial position, and two guide pins (or set screws in dedicated guide holes) prevent tilt. This is printable, reliable, and fully serviceable.\n\nThe key insight: **rigidity in a straight slot comes from the constraint system, not the slot shape.** A dovetail resists pull-out. You don't need pull-out resistance \u2014 your inserts are pushed inward by the needle butt load and retained by set screws. The force direction works in your favor with a straight slot.\n\n---\n\n## The correct straight-slot insert design\n\n```\nCAM RING BODY (cross-section, looking down Z-axis)\n\n        OUTER FACE At 60 RPM with 60 needles, each needle contacts the insert 60 times per second \u2014 micro-vibration at that frequency compounds quickly into stitch inconsistency.\n\n**Locked: 7.65mm insert width, 0.175mm clearance per side.**\nMidpoint of your range, gives 0.35mm total slot-to-insert play \u2014 tight enough for precision, loose enough to slide without binding after epoxy coat swells the PETG slightly.\n\n---\n\n## Refinement 2: Guide pins \u00d83mm \u2705\n\nAgreed completely. \u00d82mm in PETG is marginal \u2014 the pin hole wall would be under 1mm thick on each side of a 2mm pin in a 7.65mm wide insert. At \u00d83mm the pin wall thickens to ~2.3mm each side, which is genuinely structural.\n\n**Locked: \u00d83mm \u00d7 10mm hardened dowel pins, \u00d83.1mm slip holes in insert.**\n\nOne addition your collaborator didn't mention: the guide pin holes in the cam body should be **drilled or reamed after printing**, not relied on purely as-printed. A 3mm drill run by hand through the printed hole takes 30 seconds and brings the bore from \u00b10.2mm printed to \u00b10.05mm drilled \u2014 exactly what the collaborator flagged in the final warning. Mark the holes with a center punch impression in the model (small dimple feature) so drill location is unambiguous.\n\n---\n\n## Refinement 3: Back support wall \u2705\n\nThis is the most important of the three. Without a back wall, the load path under needle butt contact is:\n\n```\nneedle butt \u2192 insert active face \u2192 insert body \u2192 set screws \u2192 cam body\n```\n\nSet screws are M3 in PETG \u2014 they will creep under cyclic load within hours of operation. With a back wall:\n\n```\nneedle butt \u2192 insert active face \u2192 insert body \u2192 back wall \u2192 cam body\n```\n\nSet screws become pure locking features, not structural members. \nClearance at full inward position:\n  Insert travels -1.5mm inward (tighter stitch)  \n  Front of slot is open to cam bore \u2014 insert nose protrudes\n  No interference\n```\n\n---\n\n## Complete span)\n#   350\u00b0\u2013360\u00b0 TRANSITION   (10\u00b0 span)\n#\n# INSERT SPEC (each of 2 inserts):\n#   Arc length: 22mm | Width: 7.65mm | Height: 20mm\n#   Guide pins: 2\u00d7 \u00d83mm \u00d7 10mm (drill after print)\n#   Set screws: 2\u00d7 M3 \u00d7 6mm (radial, through outer face)\n#   Back support wall: 1.0mm behind max travel\n#   Travel: \u00b11.5mm radial | Nominal depth: 3.5mm\n# =============================================================================\n\nimport FreeCAD, Part, math\n\ndoc = FreeCAD.newDocument(\"CSM_V3_CamRing_V1_Final\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# ALL PARAMETERS \u2014 LOCKED V1 FINAL\n# =============================================================================\n\n# Geometry\nCYL_OD      = 90.0\nCAM_ID      = 90.7      # Locked R3+ (0.35mm radial gap)\nCAM_OD      = 140.0\nCAM_H       = 25.0\nCAM_WALL    = (CAM_OD - CAM_ID) / 2.0     # 24.65mm\nRAD_GAP     = (CAM_ID - CYL_OD) / 2.0    # 0.35mm\n\n# Cam zones (radial from bore)\nRAISE_Z     = 6.0    # mm radial\nGUARD_Z     = 3.0\nSTITCH_Z    = 8.0\nLOWER_Z     = 5.0\nRESERVE_Z   = CAM_WALL - RAISE_Z - GUARD_Z - STITCH_Z - LOWER_Z  # 2.65mm\n\n# Cam angular map (degrees)\nRAISE_START = 0.0;    RAISE_END  = 35.0\nHOLD_START  = 35.0;   HOLD_END   = 60.0\nSTITCH_START= 60.0;   STITCH_END = 120.0\nLOWER_START = 120.0;  LOWER_END  = 170.0\nTRANS1_END  = 180.0\n\nRAISE2_START= 180.0;  RAISE2_END = 215.0\nHOLD2_START = 215.0;  HOLD2_END  = 240.0\nSTITCH2_START=240.0;  STITCH2_END= 300.0\nLOWER2_START= 300.0;  LOWER2_END = 350.0\n\n# Cam profile heights\nNEEDLE_LIFT = 12.0   # mm \u2014 total needle raise height\nSTITCH_NOM  = 3.5    # mm \u2014 nominal stitch cam drop\nSTITCH_TRAV = 1.5    # mm \u2014 \u00b1adjustment range\n\n# Butt track\nTRACK_W     = 2.2    # mm \u2014 butt width + 0.2mm clearance\nTRACK_D     = 4.0    # mm \u2014 radial depth from bore\n\n# Insert geometry \u2014 FINAL\nINSERT_ARC  = 22.0   # mm \u2014 arc length\nINSERT_W    = 7.65   # mm \u2014 width (slot = 8.0mm, clearance 0.175mm/side)\nINSERT_H    = 20.0   # mm \u2014 height (in cam H=25, centered)\nINSERT_TRAV = 1.5    # mm \u2014 \u00b1radial travel\nBACK_WALL   = 1.0    # mm \u2014 support wall behind insert\nSLOT_W      = 8.0    # mm \u2014 slot width (insert + 2\u00d70.175mm)\n\n# Guide pins (per insert)\nPIN_D       = 3.0    # mm \u2014 \u00d83mm (upgraded from \u00d82mm)\nPIN_HOLE_D  = 3.1    # mm \u2014 slip fit H7/g6\nPIN_DEPTH   = 10.0   # mm \u2014 in cam body\nPIN_Z_1     = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0 + 4.0   # lower pin Z\nPIN_Z_2     = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0 + INSERT_H - 4.0  # upper pin Z\n\n# Cassette interface\nSHAFT_D     = 12.0\nBOLT_PCD    = 130.0\nBOLT_N      = 4\nBOLT_D      = 5.5\nKEY_W       = 3.0\nKEY_H       = 3.0\nKEY_DEPTH   = 1.5    # mm \u2014 sliding fit\n\n# Alignment pins\nAPIN_D      = 4.0\nAPIN_N      = 3\nAPIN_PCD    = 120.0\nAPIN_DEPTH  = 10.0\n\n# =============================================================================\n# DERIVED\n# =============================================================================\nCAM_R_IN  = CAM_ID / 2.0    # 45.35mm\nCAM_R_OUT = CAM_OD / 2.0    # 70.0mm\nSHAFT_R   = SHAFT_D / 2.0\nBOLT_R    = BOLT_PCD / 2.0\nAPIN_R    = APIN_PCD / 2.0\n\n# Zone radii\nR_RAISE   = CAM_R_IN + RAISE_Z    # 51.35mm\nR_GUARD   = R_RAISE + GUARD_Z     # 54.35mm\nR_STITCH  = R_GUARD + STITCH_Z    # 62.35mm\nR_LOWER   = R_STITCH + LOWER_Z    # 67.35mm\n\n# Insert pocket positions (center of arc)\nINSERT1_ANG = (STITCH_START + STITCH_END) / 2.0   # 90\u00b0\nINSERT2_ANG = (STITCH2_START + STITCH2_END) / 2.0  # 270\u00b0\n\n# Insert pocket radial position (from cam center to slot inner face)\nINSERT_R_IN  = R_GUARD          # inner face of stitch zone\nINSERT_R_OUT = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z  # = R_STITCH = 62.35mm\n\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"CSM V3 CAM RING \u2014 V1 FINAL\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\nprint(f\"OD={CAM_OD}  ID={CAM_ID}  H={CAM_H}  Wall={CAM_WALL:.2f}mm\")\nprint(f\"Radial gap: {RAD_GAP:.3f}mm (target 0.35mm) \u2705\")\nprint(f\"Cam map: 2-feed, Option B\")\nprint(f\"  Feed 1: 0\u00b0\u2013170\u00b0 | Insert #1 at {INSERT1_ANG:.0f}\u00b0\")\nprint(f\"  Feed 2: 180\u00b0\u2013350\u00b0 | Insert #2 at {INSERT2_ANG:.0f}\u00b0\")\nprint(f\"Insert: {INSERT_W}mm wide \u00d7 {INSERT_H}mm H \u00d7 {INSERT_ARC}mm arc\")\nprint(f\"  Guide pins: 2\u00d7 \u00d8{PIN_D}mm | Set screws: 2\u00d7 M3\")\nprint(f\"  Back wall: {BACK_WALL}mm | Travel: \u00b1{INSERT_TRAV}mm\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD CAM RING BODY\n# =============================================================================\nouter = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT, CAM_H)\nbore  = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_IN, CAM_H + 2)\nbore.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\ncam = outer.cut(bore)\n\n# Shaft clearance\nshft = Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_R + 2.0, CAM_H + 2)\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\ncam = cam.cut(shft)\n\n# =============================================================================\n# NEEDLE BUTT TRACK \u2014 circumferential groove on bore face\n# Needles ride in this track; cam profile modulates height\n# =============================================================================\nt_out = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_IN + TRACK_D, TRACK_W)\nt_in  = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_IN - 0.2, TRACK_W + 2)\nt_out.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0))\nt_in.translate( FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0 - 1))\ncam = cam.cut(t_out.cut(t_in))\n\n# =============================================================================\n# RAISE CAM ZONE \u2014 graduated annular pocket\n# Represents the zone where needles are pushed upward\n# Full 3D cam surface requires CNC; this defines the zone envelope\n# =============================================================================\nr_ann = Part.makeCylinder(R_RAISE, CAM_H * 0.55)\nr_inn = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_IN - 0.1, CAM_H * 0.55 + 2)\nr_ann.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H * 0.22))\nr_inn.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H * 0.22 - 1))\ncam = cam.cut(r_ann.cut(r_inn))\n\n# =============================================================================\n# STITCH CAM INSERT POCKETS \u2014 2 pockets at 90\u00b0 and 270\u00b0\n# Each pocket: straight slot with back support wall\n# =============================================================================\ninsert_z_bottom = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0   # 2.5mm from bottom\ninsert_z_top    = insert_z_bottom + INSERT_H  # 22.5mm from bottom\n\ndef make_insert_pocket(angle_deg, cam_body):\n    \"\"\"Cut insert pocket + guide pin holes + set screw holes at given angle.\"\"\"\n    ang = math.radians(angle_deg)\n    \n    # Insert slot dimensions in local coords\n    # X = tangential (arc direction), Y = radial (inward), Z = axial\n    half_arc = INSERT_ARC / 2.0\n    \n    # Main slot (full stitch zone width)\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z + BACK_WALL, INSERT_H)\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, insert_z_bottom))\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), angle_deg)\n    cam_body = cam_body.cut(slot)\n    \n    # Back support wall relief \u2014 cut only in front STITCH_Z (leave BACK_WALL)\n    # The back 1mm remains solid \u2014 this IS the back wall\n    front_slot = Part.makeBox(INSERT_ARC, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H + 2)\n    front_slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-INSERT_ARC/2, INSERT_R_IN,\n                                         insert_z_bottom - 1))\n    front_slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), angle_deg)\n    cam_body = cam_body.cut(front_slot)\n    \n    # Guide pin holes in cam body (2\u00d7 \u00d83mm, drilled after print)\n    # Position: on the cam body sides of the slot, at Z = PIN_Z_1 and PIN_Z_2\n    for pz in [PIN_Z_1, PIN_Z_2]:\n        for side in [-1, 1]:\n            pin_x = side * (SLOT_W / 2.0 + PIN_DEPTH / 2.0)\n            pin_y = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\n            ph = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_D / 2.0, PIN_DEPTH + 2)\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\n            ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(pin_x, pin_y, pz))\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), angle_deg)\n            # Only cut one pin per side (2 pins total per insert)\n            break  # one pin per Z position is sufficient\n        cam_body = cam_body.cut(ph)\n    \n    # Set screw holes (2\u00d7 M3, radial through outer cam face)\n    for arc_offset in [-INSERT_ARC / 4.0, INSERT_ARC / 4.0]:\n        ss_x = arc_offset\n        ss_y = CAM_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\n        ss = Part.makeCylinder(1.6, CAM_WALL + 2)\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\n        ss.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(ss_x, ss_y, CAM_H / 2.0))\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), angle_deg)\n        cam_body = cam_body.cut(ss)\n    \n    return cam_body\n\nprint(\"\\nCutting insert pocket #1 (90\u00b0)...\")\ncam = make_insert_pocket(INSERT1_ANG, cam)\nprint(\"Cutting insert pocket #2 (270\u00b0)...\")\ncam = make_insert_pocket(INSERT2_ANG, cam)\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BOLT HOLES (4\u00d7 M5, PCD 130mm, with countersinks)\n# =============================================================================\nfor i in range(BOLT_N):\n    ang = math.radians(i * 90.0 + 45.0)\n    bx = BOLT_R * math.cos(ang)\n    by = BOLT_R * math.sin(ang)\n    bh = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_D / 2.0, CAM_H + 2)\n    bh.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, -1))\n    cam = cam.cut(bh)\n    cs = Part.makeCylinder(4.5, 5.0)\n    cs.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, CAM_H - 5.0))\n    cam = cam.cut(cs)\n\n# =============================================================================\n# ALIGNMENT PIN HOLES (3\u00d7 \u00d84mm, H7/g6 slip, depth 10mm from bottom)\n# =============================================================================\nfor i in range(APIN_N):\n    ang = math.radians(i * 120.0 + 30.0)\n    px = APIN_R * math.cos(ang)\n    py = APIN_R * math.sin(ang)\n    ph = Part.makeCylinder(APIN_D / 2.0 + 0.05, APIN_DEPTH)\n    ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(px, py, 0))\n    cam = cam.cut(ph)\n\n# =============================================================================\n# ANTI-ROTATION KEY SLOT (sliding fit)\n# =============================================================================\nks = Part.makeBox(KEY_W + 0.15, KEY_DEPTH + 0.1, KEY_H * 5)\nks.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-(KEY_W + 0.15) / 2.0,\n                              CAM_R_IN - KEY_DEPTH - 0.1,\n                              CAM_H / 2.0 - KEY_H * 2.5))\ncam = cam.cut(ks)\n\n# =============================================================================\n# SOLENOID RESERVE GROOVE (outer face, 2mm reserve zone marking)\n# =============================================================================\nsg = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT - 0.5, 1.5)\nsi = Part.makeCylinder(R_LOWER + 0.5, 2.5)\nsg.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1.0))\nsi.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0.5))\ncam = cam.cut(sg.cut(si))\n\n# =============================================================================\n# FINALIZE\n# =============================================================================\nobj = doc.addObject(\"Part::Feature\", \"CSM_CamRing_V1_Final_2Feed\")\nobj.Shape = cam\nobj.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.28, 0.28, 0.36)\ndoc.recompute()\nFreeCAD.Gui.activeDocument().activeView().viewIsometric()\nFreeCAD.Gui.SendMsgToActiveView(\"ViewFit\")\n\nvol = cam.Volume\nprint(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\nprint(f\"\u2705 CAM RING V1 FINAL \u2014 COMPLETE\")\nprint(f\"{'='*60}\")\nprint(f\"   OD={CAM_OD}  ID={CAM_ID}  H={CAM_H}\")\nprint(f\"   Radial gap: {RAD_GAP:.3f}mm \u2705\")\nprint(f\"   Volume: {vol:.0f}mm\u00b3\")\nprint(f\"   Weight PETG: {vol*1.27/1000:.1f}g\")\nprint(f\"   Weight Al 6061: {vol*2.70/1000:.1f}g\")\nprint(f\"\\n\ud83d\udccb PRINT SETTINGS:\")\nprint(f\"   Material: PETG | Layer: 0.15mm | Perimeters: 5\")\nprint(f\"   Top/Bottom: 6 solid | Infill: 50% Gyroid\")\nprint(f\"   Orientation: FLAT (bore face UP)\")\nprint(f\"   Speed: 30mm/s walls / 150mm/s infill\")\nprint(f\"\\n\ud83d\udd34 AFTER PRINTING:\")\nprint(f\"   1. Drill guide pin holes with \u00d83mm bit (hand drill, slow)\")\nprint(f\"   2. Drill alignment pin holes with \u00d84mm bit\")\nprint(f\"   3. Sand bore + cam faces: 220\u2192400\u2192800 grit (circumferential)\")\nprint(f\"   4. Epoxy coat cam faces (West System 105+207, thin)\")\nprint(f\"   5. Cure 24h, final polish 1000\u21921500 grit\")\nprint(f\"   6. PTFE dry lube spray\")\nprint(f\"   7. Test: cylinder rotates inside with one finger\")\n\nimport os\nout = \"/mnt/user-data/outputs/3D-PROJECT/01_MECHANICAL/02_CASSETTE_HEAD/cam_ring/freecad_macros/\"\nPart.export([obj], out.replace(\"freecad_macros/\", \"\") + \"CSM_V3_CamRing_V1_Final.stl\")\nprint(f\"\\n\ud83d\udcbe STL exported\")\n",

2)\\nt_out.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0))\\nt_in.translate( FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0 - 1))\\ncam = cam.cut(t_out.cut(t_in))\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# RAISE CAM ZONE \\u2014 graduated annular pocket\\n# Represents the zone where needles are pushed upward\\n# Full 3D cam surface requires CNC; this defines the zone envelope\\n# =============================================================================\\nr_ann = Part.makeCylinder(R_RAISE, CAM_H * 0.55)\\nr_inn = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_IN - 0.1, CAM_H * 0.55 + 2)\\nr_ann.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H * 0.22))\\nr_inn.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H * 0.22 - 1))\\ncam = cam.cut(r_ann.cut(r_inn))\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# STITCH CAM INSERT POCKETS \\u2014 2 pockets at 90\\u00b0 and 270\\u00b0\\n# Each pocket: straight slot with back support wall\\n# =============================================================================\\ninsert_z_bottom = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0   # 2.5mm from bottom\\ninsert_z_top    = insert_z_bottom + INSERT_H  # 22.5mm from bottom\\n\\ndef make_insert_pocket(angle_deg, cam_body):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cut insert pocket + guide pin holes + set screw holes at given angle.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ang = math.radians(angle_deg)\\n    \\n    # Insert slot dimensions in local coords\\n    # X = tangential (arc direction), Y = radial (inward), Z = axial\\n    half_arc = INSERT_ARC / 2.0\\n    \\n    # Main slot (full stitch zone width)\\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z + BACK_WALL, INSERT_H)\\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, insert_z_bottom))\\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), angle_deg)\\n    cam_body = cam_body.cut(slot)\\n    \\n    # Back support wall relief \\u2014 cut only in front STITCH_Z (leave BACK_WALL)\\n    # The back 1mm remains solid \\u2014 this IS the back wall\\n    front_slot = Part.makeBox(INSERT_ARC, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H + 2)\\n    front_slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-INSERT_ARC/2, INSERT_R_IN,\\n                                         insert_z_bottom - 1))\\n    front_slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), angle_deg)\\n    cam_body = cam_body.cut(front_slot)\\n    \\n    # Guide pin holes in cam body (2\\u00d7 \\u00d83mm, drilled after print)\\n    # Position: on the cam body sides of the slot, at Z = PIN_Z_1 and PIN_Z_2\\n    for pz in [PIN_Z_1, PIN_Z_2]:\\n        for side in [-1, 1]:\\n            pin_x = side * (SLOT_W / 2.0 + PIN_DEPTH / 2.0)\\n            pin_y = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\\n            ph = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_D / 2.0, PIN_DEPTH + 2)\\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\\n            ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(pin_x, pin_y, pz))\\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), angle_deg)\\n            # Only cut one pin per side (2 pins total per insert)\\n            break  # one pin per Z position is sufficient\\n        cam_body = cam_body.cut(ph)\\n    \\n    # Set screw holes (2\\u00d7 M3, radial through outer cam face)\\n    for arc_offset in [-INSERT_ARC / 4.0, INSERT_ARC / 4.0]:\\n        ss_x = arc_offset\\n        ss_y = CAM_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\\n        ss = Part.makeCylinder(1.6, CAM_WALL + 2)\\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\\n        ss.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(ss_x, ss_y, CAM_H / 2.0))\\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), angle_deg)\\n        cam_body = cam_body.cut(ss)\\n    \\n    return cam_body\\n\\nprint(\\\"\\\\nCutting insert pocket #1 (90\\u00b0)...\\\")\\ncam = make_insert_pocket(INSERT1_ANG, cam)\\nprint(\\\"Cutting insert pocket #2 (270\\u00b0)...\\\")\\ncam = make_insert_pocket(INSERT2_ANG, cam)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BOLT HOLES (4\\u00d7 M5, PCD 130mm, with countersinks)\\n# =============================================================================\\nfor i in range(BOLT_N):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 90.0 + 45.0)\\n    bx = BOLT_R * math.cos(ang)\\n    by = BOLT_R * math.sin(ang)\\n    bh = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_D / 2.0, CAM_H + 2)\\n    bh.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, -1))\\n    cam = cam.cut(bh)\\n    cs = Part.makeCylinder(4.5, 5.0)\\n    cs.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, CAM_H - 5.0))\\n    cam = cam.cut(cs)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# ALIGNMENT PIN HOLES (3\\u00d7 \\u00d84mm, H7/g6 slip, depth 10mm from bottom)\\n# =============================================================================\\nfor i in range(APIN_N):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 120.0 + 30.0)\\n    px = APIN_R * math.cos(ang)\\n    py = APIN_R * math.sin(ang)\\n    ph = Part.makeCylinder(APIN_D / 2.0 + 0.05, APIN_DEPTH)\\n    ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(px, py, 0))\\n    cam = cam.cut(ph)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# ANTI-ROTATION KEY SLOT (sliding fit)\\n# =============================================================================\\nks = Part.makeBox(KEY_W + 0.15, KEY_DEPTH + 0.1, KEY_H * 5)\\nks.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-(KEY_W + 0.15) / 2.0,\\n                              CAM_R_IN - KEY_DEPTH - 0.1,\\n                              CAM_H / 2.0 - KEY_H * 2.5))\\ncam = cam.cut(ks)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# SOLENOID RESERVE GROOVE (outer face, 2mm reserve zone marking)\\n# =============================================================================\\nsg = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT - 0.5, 1.5)\\nsi = Part.makeCylinder(R_LOWER + 0.5, 2.5)\\nsg.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1.0))\\nsi.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0.5))\\ncam = cam.cut(sg.cut(si))\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# FINALIZE\\n# =============================================================================\\nobj = doc.addObject(\\\"Part::Feature\\\", \\\"CSM_CamRing_V1_Final_2Feed\\\")\\nobj.Shape = cam\\nobj.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.28, 0.28, 0.36)\\ndoc.recompute()\\nFreeCAD.Gui.activeDocument().activeView().viewIsometric()\\nFreeCAD.Gui.SendMsgToActiveView(\\\"ViewFit\\\")\\n\\nvol = cam.Volume\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*60}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\u2705 CAM RING V1 FINAL \\u2014 COMPLETE\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"{'='*60}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   OD={CAM_OD}  ID={CAM_ID}  H={CAM_H}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Radial gap: {RAD_GAP:.3f}mm \\u2705\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Volume: {vol:.0f}mm\\u00b3\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Weight PETG: {vol*1.27/1000:.1f}g\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Weight Al 6061: {vol*2.70/1000:.1f}g\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n\\ud83d\\udccb PRINT SETTINGS:\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Material: PETG | Layer: 0.15mm | Perimeters: 5\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Top/Bottom: 6 solid | Infill:
