0.2, TRACK_W + 2)\nt_out.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0))\nt_in.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0 - 1))\ncam = cam.cut(t_out.cut(t_in))\n\n# =============================================================================\n# FIX 3: INSERT POCKETS \u2014 consistent coordinate system\n# Pocket dimensions use chord (tangential) and radial (depth) correctly\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef make_insert_pocket_corrected(angle_center_deg, cam_body):\n    \"\"\"\n    Cut insert pocket at given center angle.\n    FIX 3: Uses chord dimension for tangential width (not arc length directly)\n    FIX 4: Cuts BOTH guide pin holes (no break statement)\n    FIX 5: Set screws positioned near outer face\n    \"\"\"\n    ang_rad = math.radians(angle_center_deg)\n    \n    # INSERT POCKET in local coords (centered at angle_center_deg)\n    # X = tangential (chord direction), Y = radial inward, Z = axial\n    half_chord = INSERT_CHORD / 2.0\n    radial_depth = STITCH_Z + BACK_WALL  # full zone + back wall\n    pocket_z_bot = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0\n\n    # Full slot (includes back wall material \u2014 we DON'T cut back wall)\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H)\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, pocket_z_bot))\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), angle_center_deg)\n    cam_body = cam_body.cut(slot)\n    # Back wall (BACK_WALL mm) is NOT cut \u2014 remains as support shelf\n\n    # FIX 4: BOTH guide pin holes (2 \u00d7 \u00d83mm) \u2014 NO break statement\n    # Pins run tangentially through the cam wall, flanking the insert slot\n    pin_r_pos = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0  # radial midpoint of stitch zone\n    pin_z_positions = [pocket_z_bot + 4.0, pocket_z_bot + INSERT_H - APPLIED (all 6 critical issues corrected):\\n#\\n# FIX 1 \\u2014 Functional cam surface via R(theta) profile\\n#   Was: constant-depth groove (not a cam at all)\\n#   Now: 36-point angular polygon with cosine ease-in/out lift profile\\n#        Needles will actually rise and fall with rotation\\n#\\n# FIX 2 \\u2014 Angular sector cuts, not full 360\\u00b0 cylinders\\n#   Was: Part.makeCylinder \\u2192 removes full annular ring\\n#   Now: Cam profile uses swept wire polygon (sector geometry)\\n#        Zones are correctly bounded by angle\\n#\\n# FIX 3 \\u2014 Consistent coordinate system for insert pocket\\n#   Was: Mixed arc length / box dimensions \\u2192 insert shape mismatch\\n#   Now: chord = 2*R*sin(angle/2) used correctly\\n#        Insert pocket and insert body share same coordinate system\\n#\\n# FIX 4 \\u2014 Guide pin loop bug removed\\n#   Was: break statement exited after 1 pin (only 1 of 2 pins cut)\\n#   Now: Separate holes for each pin, no break\\n#\\n# FIX 5 \\u2014 Set screw position corrected to outer face\\n#   Was: ss_y = CAM_R_IN + STITCH_Z/2 (mid-wall, unreachable)\\n#   Now: ss_y starts at CAM_R_OUT - 2.5mm (near outer face, accessible)\\n#\\n# FIX 6 \\u2014 Shaft clearance reduced\\n#   Was: SHAFT_R + 2.0mm (16mm hole, weakens structure)\\n#   Now: SHAFT_R + 0.5mm (13mm hole, correct clearance only)\\n# =============================================================================\\n\\nimport FreeCAD, Part, math\\n\\ndoc = FreeCAD.newDocument(\\\"CSM_V3_CamRing_V1_Corrected\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# LOCKED PARAMETERS \\u2014 R3+ FINAL (unchanged)\\n# =============================================================================\\n\\nCYL_OD      = 90.0\\nCAM_ID      = 90.7      # 0.35mm radial gap to cylinder\\nCAM_OD      = 140.0\\nCAM_H       = 25.0\\nCAM_WALL    = (CAM_OD - CAM_ID) / 2.0    # 24.65mm\\nRAD_GAP     = (CAM_ID - CYL_OD) / 2.0   # 0.35mm\\n\\n# Cam zones (radial width from bore inward)\\nRAISE_Z     = 6.0\\nGUARD_Z     = 3.0\\nSTITCH_Z    = 8.0\\nLOWER_Z     = 5.0\\n\\n# Cam angular map (degrees)\\nRAISE_START = 0.0;   RAISE_END  = 35.0\\nHOLD_START  = 35.0;  HOLD_END   = 60.0\\nSTITCH1_CEN = 90.0   # insert #1 center angle\\nLOWER1_END  = 170.0\\nRAISE2_START= 180.0; RAISE2_END = 215.0\\nHOLD2_END   = 240.0\\nSTITCH2_CEN = 270.0  # insert #2 center angle\\nLOWER2_END  = 350.0\\n\\n# Cam profile heights\\nNEEDLE_LIFT = 12.0   # mm \\u2014 total needle raise from neutral\\nSTITCH_DROP = 3.5    # mm \\u2014 stitch cam drop from hold\\n\\n# FIX 6: correct shaft clearance\\nSHAFT_D     = 12.0\\nSHAFT_CLEAR = SHAFT_D / 2.0 + 0.5   # 6.5mm \\u2014 was 8.0mm\\n\\n# Butt track (narrow groove on inner bore face)\\nTRACK_W     = 2.2    # mm\\nTRACK_D     = 3.0    # mm \\u2014 reduced (actual cam profile carries load)\\n\\n# FIX 3: insert dimensions with correct coordinate system\\nR_STITCH_CEN= 45.35 + 6.0 + 3.0 + 4.0  # = 58.35mm (center of stitch zone)\\nINSERT_ARC  = 22.0   # mm arc length at R_STITCH_CEN\\narc_ang_rad = INSERT_ARC / R_STITCH_CEN  # radians\\narc_ang_deg = math.degrees(arc_ang_rad)  # ~21.6\\u00b0\\nINSERT_CHORD= 2 * R_STITCH_CEN * math.sin(arc_ang_rad / 2)  # 21.87mm\\nINSERT_W    = 7.65   # mm \\u2014 slot width for insert body\\nSLOT_W      = 8.0    # mm \\u2014 pocket width (insert + 0.175mm/side)\\nINSERT_H    = 20.0   # mm \\u2014 axial height\\nINSERT_R_IN = 45.35 + 6.0 + 3.0   # start of stitch zone (= R_GUARD)\\nBACK_WALL   = 1.0    # mm \\u2014 solid rear support\\n\\n# Guide pins (FIX 4: 2 actual pins per insert)\\nPIN_D       = 3.0\\nPIN_HOLE_D  = 3.1    # slip fit\\nPIN_DEPTH   = 10.0\\n\\n# Alignment pins\\nAPIN_D      = 4.0\\nAPIN_N      = 3\\nAPIN_PCD    = 120.0\\nAPIN_DEPTH  = 10.0\\n\\n# Bolt holes\\nBOLT_PCD    = 130.0\\nBOLT_N      = 4\\nBOLT_D      = 5.5\\n\\n# Key\\nKEY_W       = 3.0\\nKEY_DEPTH   = 1.5\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# DERIVED\\n# =============================================================================\\nCAM_R_IN  = CAM_ID / 2.0    # 45.35mm\\nCAM_R_OUT = CAM_OD / 2.0    # 70.0mm\\nBOLT_R    = BOLT_PCD / 2.0\\nAPIN_R    = APIN_PCD / 2.0\\n\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\nprint(\\\"CSM V3 CAM RING V1 \\u2014 CORRECTED (all 6 fixes applied)\\\")\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\nprint(f\\\"OD={CAM_OD}  ID={CAM_ID}  H={CAM_H}  Gap={RAD_GAP:.3f}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"Shaft clearance: {SHAFT_CLEAR*2:.1f}mm hole (fix 6: was 16mm)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"Insert chord:    {INSERT_CHORD:.2f}mm (fix 3: was 22mm raw)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"Insert arc:      {arc_ang_deg:.1f}\\u00b0 at R={R_STITCH_CEN:.1f}mm\\\")\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# FIX 1 + 2: FUNCTIONAL CAM SURFACE via R(theta) polygon\\n# =============================================================================\\n# Strategy: build a 2D closed polygon (wire) in the XY plane that represents\\n# the cam inner profile as a function of angle. The profile radius varies with\\n# theta according to the needle lift curve. Extrude this wire to CAM_H.\\n# Then cut from the solid cam ring body.\\n#\\n# R(theta) = CAM_R_IN + radial_depth(theta)\\n# radial_depth > 0 = profile extends inward (pushes needle butt = lifts needle)\\n# radial_depth = 0 = neutral (no cam contact)\\n\\ndef lift_profile(theta_deg):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Returns radial cam depth at angle theta (degrees) for one feed sequence.\\n    Positive depth = cam extends inward from bore = lifts needle.\\n    Uses cosine easing for smooth acceleration/deceleration.\\n    Feed 1: 0\\u00b0-170\\u00b0  Feed 2: 180\\u00b0-350\\u00b0  Idle: remaining\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # Normalize to 0\\u00b0-180\\u00b0 for each feed\\n    if theta_deg < 180:\\n        t = theta_deg\\n        feed_offset = 0\\n    elif theta_deg < 360:\\n        t = theta_deg - 180\\n        feed_offset = 0\\n    else:\\n        return 0.0\\n\\n    if t < 0 or t > 170:\\n        return 0.0\\n    elif t <= 35:       # RAISE: cosine ease-in\\n        frac = t / 35.0\\n        return NEEDLE_LIFT * (1 - math.cos(frac * math.pi)) / 2.0\\n    elif t <= 60:       # HOLD: constant max\\n        return NEEDLE_LIFT\\n    elif t <= 120:      # STITCH DROP: cosine ease (insert controls final depth)\\n        frac = (t - 60) / 60.0\\n        return NEEDLE_LIFT - STITCH_DROP * (1 - math.cos(frac * math.pi)) / 2.0\\n    elif t <= 170:      # LOWER: cosine ease-out\\n        frac = (t - 120) / 50.0\\n        return (NEEDLE_LIFT - STITCH_DROP) * (1 - frac)**2\\n    return 0.0\\n\\n# Build cam profile polygon (72 points = 5\\u00b0 resolution)\\nN_POINTS = 72\\ncam_profile_pts = []\\nfor i in range(N_POINTS):\\n    theta = i * 360.0 / N_POINTS\\n    depth = lift_profile(theta)\\n    # Cam material must be REMOVED where needle needs to travel\\n    # So profile pocket = bore radius + depth (pocket goes deeper where needle rises)\\n    r = CAM_R_IN + depth\\n    x = r * math.cos(math.radians(theta))\\n    y = r * math.sin(math.radians(theta))\\n    cam_profile_pts.append(FreeCAD.Vector(x, y, 0))\\n\\n# Close the polygon\\ncam_profile_pts.append(cam_profile_pts[0])\\n\\n# Build wire from profile points\\nprofile_wire = Part.makePolygon(cam_profile_pts)\\n# Create face from wire\\nprofile_face = Part.Face(profile_wire)\\n# Extrude to cam height = the pocket volume to subtract\\ncam_pocket_solid = profile_face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H))\\n\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\nCam profile polygon: {N_POINTS} points at 5\\u00b0 resolution\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"Profile depth range: 0 to {NEEDLE_LIFT}mm (max lift)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"Stitch zone depth: {NEEDLE_LIFT - STITCH_DROP:.1f}mm nominal\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD CAM RING BASE BODY\\n# =============================================================================\\nouter = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT, CAM_H)\\nbore  = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_IN, CAM_H + 2)\\nbore.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\\ncam = outer.cut(bore)\\n\\n# FIX 6: correct shaft clearance\\nshft = Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_CLEAR, CAM_H + 2)\\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\\ncam = cam.cut(shft)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# APPLY FUNCTIONAL CAM SURFACE (Fix 1+2)\\n# Cut the profile pocket from the inner bore face\\n# This creates the actual varying-depth cam surface\\n# =============================================================================\\ncam = cam.cut(cam_pocket_solid)\\nprint(\\\"Cam profile applied \\u2705\\\")\\n\\n# Narrow registration groove (helps needle butt track center)\\nt_out = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_IN + TRACK_D, TRACK_W)\\nt_in  = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_IN - 0.2, TRACK_W + 2)\\nt_out.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0))\\nt_in.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0 - 1))\\ncam = cam.cut(t_out.cut(t_in))\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# FIX 3: INSERT POCKETS \\u2014 consistent coordinate system\\n# Pocket dimensions use chord (tangential) and radial (depth) correctly\\n# =============================================================================\\n\\ndef make_insert_pocket_corrected(angle_center_deg, cam_body):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Cut insert pocket at given center angle.\\n    FIX 3: Uses chord dimension for tangential width (not arc length directly)\\n    FIX 4: Cuts BOTH guide pin holes (no break statement)\\n    FIX 5: Set screws positioned near outer face\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ang_rad = math.radians(angle_center_deg)\\n    \\n    # INSERT POCKET in local coords (centered at angle_center_deg)\\n    # X = tangential (chord direction), Y = radial inward, Z = axial\\n    half_chord = INSERT_CHORD / 2.0\\n    radial_depth = STITCH_Z + BACK_WALL  # full zone + back wall\\n    pocket_z_bot = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0\\n\\n    # Full slot (includes back wall material \\u2014 we DON'T cut back wall)\\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H)\\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, pocket_z_bot))\\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), angle_center_deg)\\n    cam_body = cam_body.cut(slot)\\n    # Back wall (BACK_WALL mm) is NOT cut \\u2014 remains as support shelf\\n\\n    # FIX 4: BOTH guide pin holes (2 \\u00d7 \\u00d83mm) \\u2014 NO break statement\\n    # Pins run tangentially through the cam wall, flanking the insert slot\\n    pin_r_pos = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0  # radial midpoint of stitch zone\\n    pin_z_positions = [pocket_z_bot + 4.0, pocket_z_bot + INSERT_H - 4.0]\\n\\n    for pz in pin_z_positions:\\n        # Pin hole: tangential direction through cam wall on each side of slot\\n        for x_side in [-half_chord - PIN_DEPTH/2.0 - 0.5,\\n                        half_chord + PIN_DEPTH/2.0 + 0.5]:\\n            ph = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, PIN_DEPTH)\\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\\n            ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(x_side - PIN_DEPTH, pin_r_pos, pz))\\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), angle_center_deg)\\n            cam_body = cam_body.cut(ph)\\n\\n    # FIX 5: Set screws near OUTER face (not mid-wall)\\n    # Two screws at \\u00b11/3 of chord length, accessing from outside\\n    for chord_offset in [-half_chord * 0.5, half_chord * 0.5]:\\n        ss = Part.makeCylinder(1.6, CAM_WALL - 2.0)  # M3 tap, stops 2mm from bore\\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\\n        # Start from outer face, go inward\\n        ss_r_start = CAM_R_OUT - 0.5\\n        ss.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(chord_offset, ss_r_start - (CAM_WALL - 2.0),\\n                                     CAM_H / 2.0))\\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), angle_center_deg)\\n        cam_body = cam_body.cut(ss)\\n\\n    return cam_body\\n\\nprint(\\\"\\\\nCutting insert pocket #1 at 90\\u00b0 (Feed 1)...\\\")\\ncam = make_insert_pocket_corrected(STITCH1_CEN, cam)\\nprint(\\\"Cutting insert pocket #2 at 270\\u00b0 (Feed 2)...\\\")\\ncam = make_insert_pocket_corrected(STITCH2_CEN, cam)\\nprint(\\\"Both insert pockets complete \\u2705\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BOLT HOLES (4\\u00d7 M5 with countersinks)\\n# =============================================================================\\nfor i in range(BOLT_N):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 90.0 + 45.0)\\n    bx = BOLT_R * math.cos(ang)\\n    by = BOLT_R * math.sin(ang)\\n    bh = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_D / 2.0, CAM_H + 2)\\n    bh.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, -1))\\n    cam = cam.cut(bh)\\n    cs = Part.makeCylinder(4.5, 5.0)\\n    cs.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, CAM_H - 5.0))\\n    cam = cam.cut(cs)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# ALIGNMENT PINS (3\\u00d7 \\u00d84mm, depth 10mm, H7/g6 slip)\\n# =============================================================================\\nfor i in range(APIN_N):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 120.0 + 30.0)\\n    px = APIN_R * math.cos(ang)\\n    py = APIN_R * math.sin(ang)\\n    ph = Part.makeCylinder(APIN_D / 2.0 + 0.05, APIN_DEPTH)\\n    ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(px, py, 0))\\n    cam = cam.cut(ph)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# KEY SLOT (sliding fit)\\n# =============================================================================\\nks = Part.makeBox(KEY_W + 0.15, KEY_DEPTH + 0.1, KEY_W * 5)\\nks.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-(KEY_W + 0.15) / 2.0,\\n                              CAM_R_IN - KEY_DEPTH - 0.1,\\n                              CAM_H / 2.0 - KEY_W * 2.5))\\ncam = cam.cut(ks)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# FINALIZE\\n# =============================================================================\\nobj = doc.addObject(\\\"Part::Feature\\\", \\\"CSM_CamRing_V1_Corrected\\\")\\nobj.Shape = cam\\nobj.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.28, 0.28, 0.36)\\ndoc.recompute()\\nFreeCAD.Gui.activeDocument().activeView().viewIsometric()\\nFreeCAD.Gui.SendMsgToActiveView(\\\"ViewFit\\\")\\n\\nvol = cam.Volume\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*60}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\u2705 CAM RING V1 CORRECTED \\u2014 COMPLETE\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"{'='*60}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   OD={CAM_OD}  ID={CAM_ID}  H={CAM_H}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Volume: {vol:.0f}mm\\u00b3  PETG: {vol*1.27/1000:.1f}g  Al: {vol*2.70/1000:.1f}g\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n6 FIXES CONFIRMED:\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   \\u2705 Fix 1: Functional R(theta) cam profile (72-point polygon)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   \\u2705 Fix 2: Angular sector geometry (no full-ring cuts)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   \\u2705 Fix 3: Chord dimensions for insert pocket\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   \\u2705 Fix 4: Both guide pin holes cut (no break)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   \\u2705 Fix 5: Set screws at outer face (CAM_R_OUT - 2.5mm)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   \\u2705 Fix 6: Shaft clearance = {SHAFT_CLEAR*2:.0f}mm (was 16mm)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n\\ud83d\\udccb PRINT SETTINGS:\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Material: PETG | Layer: 0.15mm | Perimeters: 5\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Orientation: FLAT | Speed: 30mm/s walls\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n\\ud83d\\udd34 MANDATORY POST-PROCESS:\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Sand cam profile face 220\\u2192400\\u2192800 grit (circumferential)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Thin epoxy coat \\u2192 cure \\u2192 1000\\u21921500 wet sand \\u2192 PTFE lube\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Drill pin holes after print: \\u00d83mm guide + \\u00d84mm alignment\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n\\u26a0\\ufe0f  CAM PROFILE NOTE:\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   R(theta) profile uses 72-point cosine easing polygon\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Stitch depth controlled by insert, not profile floor\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   For aluminum final: CNC this profile to \\u00b10.05mm tolerance\\\")\\n\\nimport os, Part as P\\nout = \\\"C:/3D-Project/01_MECHANICAL/02_CASSETTE_HEAD/cam_ring/freecad_macros/\\\"\\n# STL for local save:\\nlocal_stl = os.path.expanduser(\\\"~/CSM_PROJECT/CamRing_V1_Corrected.stl\\\")\\nP.export([obj], local_stl)\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n\\ud83d\\udcbe STL saved to: {local_stl}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Copy to: {out}\\\")\\n\", \"filename\": \"/mnt/user-data/outputs/3D-PROJECT/01_MECHANICAL/02_CASSETTE_HEAD/cam_ring/freecad_macros/CSM_V3_CamRing_V1_Corrected.FCMacro\"}"

OPTION A \u2014 CONSERVATIVE CAM (RECOMMENDED FIRST)\n   * Longer ramps \u2192 smooth motion\n   * Lower impact \u2192 ideal for PETG prototype\n   * Stable at 60\u2013120 RPM\n\n```\n# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\n# CSM V3 \u2014 CAM RING (CONSERVATIVE PROFILE)\n\nimport FreeCAD, Part, math\n\ndoc = FreeCAD.newDocument(\"Cam_Conservative\")\n\n# ---------------- PARAMETERS ----------------\nCAM_ID = 90.7\nCAM_OD = 140.0\nCAM_H  = 25.0\n\nR_BASE = CAM_ID / 2.0\n\n# Conservative motion\nLIFT = 10.0      # total lift (mm)\nSTITCH_DROP = 3.2\n\nPOINTS = 144\n\n# Angular zones (per feed)\ndef cam_radius(theta_deg):\n    t = theta_deg % 180.0  # repeat every 180\u00b0 (2-feed)\n\n    # RAISE (0\u201350\u00b0)\n    if t < 50:\n        x = t / 50.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT * (1 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2\n\n    # HOLD (50\u201380\u00b0)\n    elif t < 80:\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\n\n    # STITCH (80\u2013140\u00b0)\n    elif t < 140:\n        x = (t - 80) / 60.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\n\n    # LOWER (140\u2013170\u00b0)\n    elif t < 170:\n        x = (t - 140) / 30.0\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2)\n\n    # TRANSITION\n    else:\n        return R_BASE\n\n# ---------------- BUILD PROFILE ----------------\npts = []\nfor i in range(POINTS):\n    ang = 2 * math.pi * i / POINTS\n    deg = math.degrees(ang)\n    r = cam_radius(deg)\n    pts.append(FreeCAD.Vector(r * math.cos(ang), r * math.sin(ang), 0))\n\npts.append(pts[0])\n\nwire = Part.makePolygon(pts)\nface = Part.Face(wire)\n\ncam_track = face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H))\n\nouter = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_OD/2, CAM_H)\ncam = outer.cut(cam_track)\n\nobj = doc.addObject(\"Part::Feature\", \"Cam_Conservative\")\nobj.Shape = cam\ndoc.recompute()\n```\n\n\ud83d\udd34 OPTION B \u2014 AGGRESSIVE CAM\n   *  Shorter ramps \n   *  Faster needle motion \n   *  Higher stress (NOT ideal for raw PETG without finishing) \n\n```\n# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\n# CSM V3 \u2014 CAM RING (AGGRESSIVE PROFILE)\n\nimport FreeCAD, Part, math\n\ndoc = FreeCAD.newDocument(\"Cam_Aggressive\")\n\n# ---------------- PARAMETERS ----------------\nCAM_ID = 90.7\nCAM_OD = 140.0\nCAM_H  = 25.0\n\nR_BASE = CAM_ID / 2.0\n\n# Aggressive motion\nLIFT = 12.0\nSTITCH_DROP = 3.8\n\nPOINTS = 144\n\ndef cam_radius(theta_deg):\n    t = theta_deg % 180.0\n\n    # RAISE (0\u201330\u00b0) steeper\n    if t < 30:\n        x = t / 30.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT * (1 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2\n\n    # HOLD (30\u201360\u00b0)\n    elif t < 60:\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\n\n    # STITCH (60\u2013120\u00b0)\n    elif t < 120:\n        x = (t - 60) / 60.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\n\n    # LOWER (120\u2013150\u00b0)\n    elif t < 150:\n        x = (t - 120) / 30.0\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2)\n\n    else:\n        return R_BASE\n\n# ---------------- BUILD PROFILE ----------------\npts = []\nfor i in range(POINTS):\n    ang = 2 * math.pi * i / POINTS\n    deg = math.degrees(ang)\n    r = cam_radius(deg)\n    pts.append(FreeCAD.Vector(r * math.cos(ang), r * math.sin(ang), 0))\n\npts.append(pts[0])\n\nwire = Part.makePolygon(pts)\nface = Part.Face(wire)\n\ncam_track = face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H))\n\nouter = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_OD/2, CAM_H)\ncam = outer.cut(cam_track)\n\nobj = doc.addObject(\"Part::Feature\", \"Cam_Aggressive\")\nobj.Shape = cam\ndoc.recompute()\n```\n\n\u26a0\ufe0f Important Observations\n1. Conservative vs Aggressive behavior\nFeatureConservativeAggressiveRaise angleshallowsteepNeedle stresslowhighRPM capabilitystablehigher potentialPETG compatibility\u2705 safe\u26a0\ufe0f risky",

= 2.1     # mm \u2014 butt width 2.0mm + 0.10mm total clearance\nTRACK_D     = 2.5     # mm \u2014 narrow: cam profile carries the load\n\n# Insert geometry \u2014 corrected coordinate =============================================================================\n# R(theta) \u2014 CONSERVATIVE CAM PROFILE FUNCTION\n# =============================================================================\ndef cam_radius(theta_deg):\n    \"\"\"\n    Returns cam inner radius at angle theta_deg.\n    2-feed: profile repeats every 180\u00b0.\n    R > R_BASE means cam extends inward \u2192 pushes needle butt \u2192 lifts needle.\n\n    Conservative zones (per 180\u00b0 feed cycle):\n      RAISE   0\u00b0\u2013 50\u00b0  cosine ease-in  (shallow, low stress)\n      HOLD   50\u00b0\u2013 80\u00b0  constant max\n      STITCH 80\u00b0\u2013140\u00b0  linear drop (fine-tuned by insert)\n      LOWER 140\u00b0\u2013170\u00b0  cosine ease-out (smooth return)\n      IDLE  170\u00b0\u2013180\u00b0  neutral\n    \"\"\"\n    t = theta_deg % 180.0   # normalize to one feed cycle\n\n    if t < 50.0:            # RAISE \u2014 cosine ease-in\n        x = t / 50.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT * (1 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\n\n    elif t < 80.0:          # HOLD \u2014 constant max\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\n\n    elif t < 140.0:         # STITCH \u2014 linear drop (insert adjusts final depth)\n        x = (t - 80.0) / 60.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\n\n    elif t < 170.0:         # LOWER \u2014 cosine ease-out (prevents butt bounce)\n        x = (t - 140.0) / 30.0\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT - STITCH_DROP) * (1 - x)**2\n\n    else:                   # IDLE / TRANSITION\n        return R_BASE\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD 144-POINT CAM PROFILE POLYGON\n# # close polygon\n\nwire = Part.makePolygon(pts)\nface = Part.Face(wire)\ncam_profile_solid = face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H))\n\n# Diagnostic: print profile at key angles\nprint(f\"  R at R_BASE + {LIFT}mm)\")\nprint(f\"  R at  65\u00b0 (hold):     {cam_radius(65):.2f}mm\")\nprint(f\"  R at 110\u00b0 (stitch):   {cam_radius(110):.2f}mm  (dropping)\")\nprint(f\"  R at 155\u00b0 (lower):    {cam_radius(155):.2f}mm\")\nprint(f\"  R at 175\u00b0 (idle):     {cam_radius(175):.2f}mm  (= R_BASE neutral)\")\n\n# outer.cut(cam_profile_solid)\n\nprint(f\"  Cam ring base: OD={CAM_OD}mm, H={CAM_H}mm\")\nprint(f\"  Profile volume removed correctly\")\n\n# Shaft clearance (Fix 6: correct size)\nshft =  # HOLD \\u2014 constant max\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\\n\\n    elif t < 140.0:         # STITCH \\u2014 linear drop (insert adjusts final depth)\\n        x = (t - 80.0) / 60.0\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\\n\\n    elif t < 170.0:         # LOWER \\u2014 cosine ease-out (prevents butt bounce)\\n        x = (t - 140.0) / 30.0\\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT - STITCH_DROP) * (1 - x)**2\\n\\n    else:                   # IDLE / TRANSITION\\n        return R_BASE\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD 144-POINT CAM PROFILE POLYGON\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"Building R(theta) cam profile...\\\")\\npts = []\\nfor i in range(POINTS):\\n    ang_rad = 2 * math.pi * i / POINTS\\n    ang_deg = math.degrees(ang_rad)\\n    r = cam_radius(ang_deg)\\n    pts.append(FreeCAD.Vector(\\n        r * math.cos(ang_rad),\\n        r * math.sin(ang_rad),\\n        0\\n    ))\\npts.append(pts[0])   # close polygon\\n\\nwire = Part.makePolygon(pts)\\nface = Part.Face(wire)\\ncam_profile_solid = face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H))\\n\\n# Diagnostic: print profile at key angles\\nprint(f\\\"  R at   0\\u00b0 (start):    {cam_radius(0):.2f}mm  (= R_BASE neutral)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  R at  25\\u00b0 (mid-raise):{cam_radius(25):.2f}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  R at  50\\u00b0 (peak):     {cam_radius(50):.2f}mm  (= R_BASE + {LIFT}mm)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  R at  65\\u00b0 (hold):     {cam_radius(65):.2f}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  R at 110\\u00b0 (stitch):   {cam_radius(110):.2f}mm  (dropping)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  R at 155\\u00b0 (lower):    {cam_radius(155):.2f}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  R at 175\\u00b0 (idle):     {cam_radius(175):.2f}mm  (= R_BASE neutral)\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD CAM RING BODY \\u2014 cut with real cam profile\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"\\\\nBuilding cam ring body...\\\")\\nouter = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT, CAM_H)\\ncam   = outer.cut(cam_profile_solid)\\n\\nprint(f\\\"  Cam ring base: OD={CAM_OD}mm, H={CAM_H}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Profile volume removed correctly\\\")\\n\\n# Shaft clearance (Fix 6: correct size)\\nshft = Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_CLEAR, CAM_H + 2)\\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\\ncam = cam.cut(shft)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# REGISTRATION GROOVE \\u2014 narrow, for needle butt lateral guidance only\\n# Track width 2.1mm = butt 2.0mm + 0.10mm total clearance (corrected)\\n# =============================================================================\\nt_out = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE + TRACK_D, TRACK_W)\\nt_in  = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE - 0.1, TRACK_W + 2)\\nt_out.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0))\\nt_in.translate( FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0 - 1))\\ncam = cam.cut(t_out.cut(t_in))\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# INSERT POCKETS \\u2014 both at 90\\u00b0 and 270\\u00b0 (Fix 3+4+5 all applied)\\n# =============================================================================\\ndef make_insert_pocket(center_ang_deg, cam_body):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Cut stitch cam insert pocket at center_ang_deg.\\n    Fix 3: chord dimension used (not raw arc length)\\n    Fix 4: both guide pin holes cut (no break)\\n    Fix 5: set screws positioned at outer face\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    half_chord = INSERT_CHORD / 2.0\\n    pz_bot = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0   # 2.5mm from bottom\\n\\n    # Main slot (stitch zone depth only \\u2014 back wall remains solid)\\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H)\\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, pz_bot))\\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n    cam_body = cam_body.cut(slot)\\n\\n    # Guide pins: 2 per insert, both Z positions (Fix 4 \\u2014 no break)\\n    pin_r = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\\n    for pz in [pz_bot + 4.0, pz_bot + INSERT_H - 4.0]:\\n        ph = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, PIN_DEPTH + 2)\\n        ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\\n        ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\\n            -half_chord - PIN_DEPTH - 1,\\n            pin_r,\\n            pz\\n        ))\\n        ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n        cam_body = cam_body.cut(ph)\\n\\n    # Set screws: 2\\u00d7 M3, from outer face inward (Fix 5)\\n    for chord_off in [-half_chord * 0.5, half_chord * 0.5]:\\n        ss = Part.makeCylinder(1.6, CAM_WALL - 2.0)\\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\\n        # Start from outer face\\n        ss.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\\n            chord_off,\\n            CAM_R_OUT - (CAM_WALL - 2.0) - 0.5,\\n            CAM_H / 2.0\\n        ))\\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n        cam_body = cam_body.cut(ss)\\n\\n    return cam_body\\n\\nprint(\\\"\\\\nCutting insert pockets...\\\")\\ncam = make_insert_pocket(STITCH1_ANG, cam)   # Feed 1 at 90\\u00b0\\nprint(\\\"  Insert #1 (90\\u00b0) done\\\")\\ncam = make_insert_pocket(STITCH2_ANG, cam)   # Feed 2 at 270\\u00b0\\nprint(\\\"  Insert #2 (270\\u00b0) done\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BOLT HOLES \\u2014 4\\u00d7 M5 with countersinks\\n# =============================================================================\\nfor i in range(BOLT_N):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 90.0 + 45.0)\\n    bx = BOLT_R * math.cos(ang)\\n    by = BOLT_R * math.sin(ang)\\n    bh = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_D / 2.0, CAM_H + 2)\\n    bh.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, -1))\\n    cam = cam.cut(bh)\\n    cs = Part.makeCylinder(4.5, 5.0)\\n    cs.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, CAM_H - 5.0))\\n    cam = cam.cut(cs)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# ALIGNMENT PINS \\u2014 3\\u00d7 \\u00d84mm, H7/g6 slip, 10mm depth\\n# =============================================================================\\nfor i in range(APIN_N):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 120.0 + 30.0)\\n    px = APIN_R * math.cos(ang)\\n    py = APIN_R * math.sin(ang)\\n    ph = Part.makeCylinder(APIN_D / 2.0 + 0.05, APIN_DEPTH)\\n    ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(px, py, 0))\\n    cam = cam.cut(ph)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# KEY SLOT \\u2014 sliding fit (not press)\\n# =============================================================================\\nks = Part.makeBox(KEY_W + 0.15, KEY_DEPTH + 0.1, KEY_W * 5)\\nks.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\\n    -(KEY_W + 0.15) / 2.0,\\n    R_BASE - KEY_DEPTH - 0.1,\\n    CAM_H / 2.0 - KEY_W * 2.5\\n))\\ncam = cam.cut(ks)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# SOLENOID RESERVE GROOVE (outer face \\u2014 marks future upgrade zone)\\n# =============================================================================\\nsg = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT - 0.5, 1.5)\\nsi = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT - 3.0, 2.5)\\nsg.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1.0))\\nsi.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0.5))\\ncam = cam.cut(sg.cut(si))\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# FINALIZE\\n# =============================================================================\\nobj = doc.addObject(\\\"Part::Feature\\\", \\\"CSM_CamRing_Conservative_V1\\\")\\nobj.Shape = cam\\nobj.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.22, 0.35, 0.55)   # blue \\u2014 conservative\\ndoc.recompute()\\n\\nFreeCAD.Gui.activeDocument().activeView().viewIsometric()\\nFreeCAD.Gui.SendMsgToActiveView(\\\"ViewFit\\\")\\n\\nvol = cam.Volume\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*60}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\u2705 CSM V3 CAM RING \\u2014 CONSERVATIVE PROFILE V1 FINAL\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"{'='*60}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Profile:  Conservative (Option A)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   OD={CAM_OD}  ID={CAM_ID}  H={CAM_H}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Lift={LIFT}mm  StitchDrop={STITCH_DROP}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Points={POINTS} (2.5\\u00b0/step)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\" Al={vol*2.70/1000:.1f}g\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n\\ud83d\\udccb PROFILE COMPARISON:\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"   Conservative (this file): Raise 0\\u00b0\\u201350\\u00b0  PETG safe  60\\u2013120 R=55.283mm  depth=9.933mm  [stitch mid]\n#   \u03b8=140\u00b0  R=53.150mm  depth=7.800mm  [LOWER start]\n#   \u03b8=155\u00b0  R=49.250mm  depth=3.900mm  [lower mid]\n#   \u03b8=170\u00b0  R=45.350mm  depth=0.000mm  [IDLE neutral]\n# =============================================================================\n\nimport FreeCAD, Part, math\n\ndoc = =============================================================================\n\nCAM_ID      = 90.7       # mm \u2014 LOCKED (0.35mm radial gap to cylinder)\nCAM_OD      = 140.0      # mm \u2014 neutral radius (no cam contact)\nCAM_R_OUT   = CAM_OD / 2.0   # 70.0mm\n\n# Conservative profile \u2014 V2 values (all 4 fixes applied)\nLIFT        = 11.0   # mm \u2014 FIX 2: was 10.0mm (too low for loop clearing)\nSTITCH_DROP = 3.2    # mm \u2014 nominal stitch cam depth (insert adjusts \u00b11.5mm)\nPOINTS      = 288    # FIX 4: was 144 (chord error now ~0\u00b5m)\n\n# Cam zone radial widths\nRAISE_Z     = 6.0    # mm radial\nGUARD_Z     = 3.0\nSTITCH_Z    = 8.0\nLOWER_Z     = 5.0\n\n# Track groove \u2014 FIX 3\nTRACK_W     = 2.1    # mm \u2014 butt 2.0mm + 0.10mm total (measure real needle!)\nTRACK_D     = 3.3    # mm \u2014 FIX 3: was 2.5mm (lateral stability at speed)\n\n# Insert geometry \u2014 corrected coordinate system (chord, not arc)\nR_STITCH_CEN = R_BASE + RAISE_Z + GUARD_Z + STITCH_Z / 2.0  # 58.35mm\nINSERT_ARC   = 22.0   # mm arc length\narc_ang_rad  = INSERT_ARC / R_STITCH_CEN\nINSERT_CHORD = 2 * R_STITCH_CEN * math.sin(arc_ang_rad / 2)  # ~21.87mm\nINSERT_W     = 7.65   # mm \u2014 insert body width\nSLOT_W       = 8.0    # mm \u2014 pocket width (0.175mm clearance/side)\nINSERT_H     = 20.0   # mm \u2014 axial height\nINSERT_R_IN  = R_BASE + RAISE_Z + GUARD_Z   # 54.35mm \u2014 inner face of stitch zone\nBACK_WALL    = 1.0    # mm \u2014 rear support wall (NOT cut)\nSTITCH1_ANG  = 90.0   # \u00b0 \u2014 insert #1 center (Feed 1)\nSTITCH2_ANG  = 270.0  # \u00b0 \u2014 insert #2 center (Feed 2)\n\n# Guide pins \u2014 \u00d83mm slip fit\nPIN_D        = 3.0\nPIN_HOLE_D   = 3.1    # H7/g6\nPIN_DEPTH    = 10.0\n\n# Cassette interface \u2014 all locked\nSHAFT_CLEAR  = 12.0 / 2.0 + 0.5   # 6.5mm radius (13mm hole)\nBOLT_PCD     = 130.0\nBOLT_N       = 4\nBOLT_D       = 5.5\nAPIN_PCD     = 120.0\nAPIN_N       = 3\nAPIN_D       = 4.0\nAPIN_DEPTH   = 10.0\nKEY_W        = 3.0\nKEY_DEPTH    = 1.5\n\nBOLT_R  = BOLT_PCD / 2.0\nAPIN_R  = APIN_PCD / 2.0\n\n# =============================================================================\n# R(theta) \u2014 CONSERVATIVE CAM PROFILE  V2\n# All 4 fixes applied. Continuous, smooth, no step changes.\n# =============================================================================\ndef cam_radius(theta_deg):\n    \"\"\"\n    Conservative cam profile for 12G latch needles at 60\u2013120 RPM on PETG cam.\n    Returns inner radius of cam at angle theta_deg.\n    R > R_BASE: cam extends inward, pushes needle butt up, lifts needle.\n    2-feed: profile repeats every 180\u00b0.\n    \"\"\"\n    t = theta_deg % 180.0\n\n    if t < 50.0:\n        # RAISE \u2014 cosine ease-in (smooth acceleration, minimal butt shock)\n        x = t / 50.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT * (1.0 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\n\n    elif t < 80.0:\n        # HOLD \u2014 constant max (needle fully raised, yarn enters hook)\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\n\n    elif t < 140.0:\n        # STITCH \u2014 linear drop (insert fine-tunes final stitch depth)\n        x = (t - 80.0) / 60.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\n\n    elif t < 170.0:\n        # LOWER \u2014 FIX 1: symmetric cosine ease-out (was quadratic \u2014 too steep)\n        # Symmetric cosine matches raise profile, eliminates butt impact\n        x = (t - 140.0) / 30.0\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT - STITCH_DROP) * (1.0 + math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\n\n    else:\n        # IDLE / TRANSITION \u2014 neutral (no cam contact)\n        return R_BASE\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD 288-POINT CAM PROFILE POLYGON\n# =============================================================================\nprint(\"=\" * 55)\nprint(\"CSM V3 CAM RING \u2014 CONSERVATIVE V2\")\nprint(\"=\" * 55)\nprint(f\"  Lift={LIFT}mm  Drop={STITCH_DROP}mm  Points={POINTS}\")\nprint(f\"  CAM_ID={CAM_ID}mm  CAM_OD={CAM_OD}mm  H={CAM_H}mm\")\nprint(f\"  Track: W={TRACK_W}mm D={TRACK_D}mm\")\nprint()\nprint(\"Building profile polygon...\")\n\npts = []\nfor i in range(POINTS):\n    ang_rad = 2.0 * math.pi * i / POINTS\n    ang_deg = math.degrees(ang_rad)\n    r = cam_radius(ang_deg)\n    pts.append(FreeCAD.Vector(\n        r * math.cos(ang_rad),\n        r * math.sin(ang_rad),\n        0.0\n    ))\npts.append(pts[0])  # close\n\nwire = Part.makePolygon(pts)\nface = Part.Face(wire)\nprofile_solid = face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H))\nprint(f\"  Profile solid: {POINTS} points, 1.25\u00b0/step\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD OUTER RING AND SUBTRACT PROFILE\n# =============================================================================\nouter = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT, CAM_H)\ncam   = outer.cut(profile_solid)\nprint(\"  Outer ring - profile = cam body \u2705\")\n\n# Shaft clearance (13mm hole \u2014 not structural load path)\nshft = Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_CLEAR, CAM_H + 2)\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\ncam = cam.cut(shft)\n\n# =============================================================================\n# REGISTRATION GROOVE \u2014 lateral butt guidance (not load bearing)\n# FIX 3: TRACK_D=3.3mm prevents butt climbing at speed\n# =============================================================================\nt_out = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE + TRACK_D, TRACK_W)\nt_in  = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE - 0.1, TRACK_W + 2)\nt_out.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0))\nt_in.translate( FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0 - 1))\ncam = cam.cut(t_out.cut(t_in))\nprint(f\"  Registration groove: W={TRACK_W}mm D={TRACK_D}mm \u2705\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# INSERT POCKETS \u2014 Feed 1 at 90\u00b0, Feed 2 at 270\u00b0\n# =============================================================================\ndef make_insert_pocket(center_ang_deg, cam_body):\n    half_chord = INSERT_CHORD / 2.0\n    pz_bot = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0  # 2.5mm from bottom\n\n    # Main slot \u2014 stitch zone width, back wall remains solid\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H)\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, pz_bot))\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\n    cam_body = cam_body.cut(slot)\n\n    # Guide pin holes: 2 per insert, both Z positions (no break \u2014 Fix 4 retained)\n    pin_r = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\n    for pz in [pz_bot + 4.0, pz_bot + INSERT_H - 4.0]:\n        ph = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, PIN_DEPTH + 2)\n        ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\n        ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\n            -half_chord - PIN_DEPTH - 1,\n            pin_r, pz\n        ))\n        ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\n        cam_body = cam_body.cut(ph)\n\n    # Set screws: 2\u00d7 M3 from outer face (Fix 5 retained)\n    for chord_off in [-half_chord * 0.5, half_chord * 0.5]:\n        ss = Part.makeCylinder(1.6, CAM_WALL - 2.0)\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\n        ss.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\n            chord_off,\n            CAM_R_OUT - (CAM_WALL - 2.0) - 0.5,\n            CAM_H / 2.0\n        ))\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\n        cam_body = cam_body.cut(ss)\n\n    return cam_body\n\ncam = make_insert_pocket(STITCH1_ANG, cam)\nprint(\"  Insert pocket #1 at 90\u00b0 \u2705\")\ncam = make_insert_pocket(STITCH2_ANG, cam)\nprint(\"  Insert pocket #2 at 270\u00b0 \u2705\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BOLT HOLES \u2014 4\u00d7 M5 with countersinks\n# =============================================================================\nfor i in range(BOLT_N):\n    ang = math.radians(i * 90.0 + 45.0)\n    bx, by = BOLT_R * math.cos(ang), BOLT_R * math.sin(ang)\n    bh = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_D / 2.0, CAM_H + 2)\n    bh.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, -1))\n    cam = cam.cut(bh)\n    cs = Part.makeCylinder(4.5, 5.0)\n    cs.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, CAM_H - 5.0))\n    cam = cam.cut(cs)\n\n# =============================================================================\n# ALIGNMENT PINS \u2014 3\u00d7 \u00d84mm, 10mm depth, H7/g6 slip\n# =============================================================================\nfor i in range(APIN_N):\n    ang = math.radians(i * 120.0 + 30.0)\n    px, py = APIN_R * math.cos(ang), APIN_R * math.sin(ang)\n    ph = Part.makeCylinder(APIN_D / 2.0 + 0.05, APIN_DEPTH)\n    ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(px, py, 0))\n    cam = cam.cut(ph)\n\n# =============================================================================\n# KEY SLOT \u2014 sliding fit\n# =============================================================================\nks = Part.makeBox(KEY_W + 0.15, KEY_DEPTH + 0.1, KEY_W * 5)\nks.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\n    -(KEY_W + 0.15) / 2.0,\n    R_BASE - deceleration, eliminates butt impact/clicking\\n#\\n#   FIX 2 \\u2014 LIFT increased 10.0 \\u2192 11.0mm\\n#     12G latch needles typically need 10.5\\u201312mm for complete loop clearing\\n#     11.0mm: safe compromise, still conservative, avoids dropped stitches\\n#\\n#   FIX 3 \\u2014 TRACK_D increased 2.5 \\u2192 3.3mm\\n#     At 60\\u2013120 RPM needle butts need lateral stability during raise\\n#     3.3mm prevents butt climbing out of track at speed\\n#\\n#   FIX 4 \\u2014 POINTS increased 144 \\u2192 288\\n#     288 pts = 1.25\\u00b0/step: chord error ~0\\u00b5m (was 0.2\\u00b5m at 144 pts)\\n#     Smoother cam surface, less manual sanding required\\n#\\n# CONSERVATIVE PROFILE PARAMETERS:\\n#   Raise:  0\\u00b0\\u201350\\u00b0   cosine ease-in  (50\\u00b0 vs 30\\u00b0 aggressive)\\n#   Hold:   50\\u00b0\\u201380\\u00b0  constant\\n#   Stitch: 80\\u00b0\\u2013140\\u00b0 linear (controlled by insert \\u00b11.5mm)\\n#   Lower:  140\\u00b0\\u2013170\\u00b0 cosine ease-out (FIXED symmetric)\\n#   Idle:   170\\u00b0\\u2013180\\u00b0 neutral\\n#   2-feed: profile repeats at 180\\u00b0 \\u2014 inserts at 90\\u00b0 and 270\\u00b0\\n#\\n# FULL PROFILE (key angles):\\n#   \\u03b8=  0\\u00b0  R=45.350mm  depth=0.000mm  [RAISE start]\\n#   \\u03b8= 25\\u00b0  R=50.863mm  depth=5.513mm  [mid-raise]\\n#   \\u03b8= 50\\u00b0  R=56.350mm  depth=11.000mm [HOLD peak]\\n#   \\u03b8= 80\\u00b0  R=56.350mm  depth=11.000mm [STITCH start]\\n#   \\u03b8=110\\u00b0  R=55.283mm  depth=9.933mm  [stitch mid]\\n#   \\u03b8=140\\u00b0  R=53.150mm  depth=7.800mm  [LOWER start]\\n#   \\u03b8=155\\u00b0  R=49.250mm  depth=3.900mm  [lower mid]\\n#   \\u03b8=170\\u00b0  R=45.350mm  depth=0.000mm  [IDLE neutral]\\n# =============================================================================\\n\\nimport FreeCAD, Part, math\\n\\ndoc = FreeCAD.newDocument(\\\"CSM_CamRing_Conservative_V2\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# LOCKED PARAMETERS \\u2014 ALL CONFIRMED\\n# =============================================================================\\n\\nCAM_ID      = 90.7       # mm \\u2014 LOCKED (0.35mm radial gap to cylinder)\\nCAM_OD      = 140.0      # mm \\u2014 LOCKED\\nCAM_H       = 25.0       # mm \\u2014 LOCKED\\nCYL_OD      = 90.0       # mm \\u2014 LOCKED\\nCAM_WALL    = (CAM_OD - CAM_ID) / 2.0   # 24.65mm\\n\\nR_BASE      = CAM_ID / 2.0    # 45.35mm \\u2014 neutral radius (no cam contact)\\nCAM_R_OUT   = CAM_OD / 2.0   # 70.0mm\\n\\n# Conservative profile \\u2014 V2 values (all 4 fixes applied)\\nLIFT        = 11.0   # mm \\u2014 FIX 2: was 10.0mm (too low for loop clearing)\\nSTITCH_DROP = 3.2    # mm \\u2014 nominal stitch cam depth (insert adjusts \\u00b11.5mm)\\nPOINTS      = 288    # FIX 4: was 144 (chord error now ~0\\u00b5m)\\n\\n# Cam zone radial widths\\nRAISE_Z     = 6.0    # mm radial\\nGUARD_Z     = 3.0\\nSTITCH_Z    = 8.0\\nLOWER_Z     = 5.0\\n\\n# Track groove \\u2014 FIX 3\\nTRACK_W     = 2.1    # mm \\u2014 butt 2.0mm + 0.10mm total (measure real needle!)\\nTRACK_D     = 3.3    # mm \\u2014 FIX 3: was 2.5mm (lateral stability at speed)\\n\\n# Insert geometry \\u2014 corrected coordinate system (chord, not arc)\\nR_STITCH_CEN = R_BASE + RAISE_Z + GUARD_Z + STITCH_Z / 2.0  # 58.35mm\\nINSERT_ARC   = 22.0   # mm arc length\\narc_ang_rad  = INSERT_ARC / R_STITCH_CEN\\nINSERT_CHORD = 2 * R_STITCH_CEN * math.sin(arc_ang_rad / 2)  # ~21.87mm\\nINSERT_W     = 7.65   # mm \\u2014 insert body width\\nSLOT_W       = 8.0    # mm \\u2014 pocket width (0.175mm clearance/side)\\nINSERT_H     = 20.0   # mm \\u2014 axial height\\nINSERT_R_IN  = R_BASE + RAISE_Z + GUARD_Z   # 54.35mm \\u2014 inner face of stitch zone\\nBACK_WALL    = 1.0    # mm \\u2014 rear support wall (NOT cut)\\nSTITCH1_ANG  = 90.0   # \\u00b0 \\u2014 insert #1 center (Feed 1)\\nSTITCH2_ANG  = 270.0  # \\u00b0 \\u2014 insert #2 center (Feed 2)\\n\\n# Guide pins \\u2014 \\u00d83mm slip fit\\nPIN_D        = 3.0\\nPIN_HOLE_D   = 3.1    # H7/g6\\nPIN_DEPTH    = 10.0\\n\\n# Cassette interface \\u2014 all locked\\nSHAFT_CLEAR  = 12.0 / 2.0 + 0.5   # 6.5mm radius (13mm hole)\\nBOLT_PCD     = 130.0\\nBOLT_N       = 4\\nBOLT_D       = 5.5\\nAPIN_PCD     = 120.0\\nAPIN_N       = 3\\nAPIN_D       = 4.0\\nAPIN_DEPTH   = 10.0\\nKEY_W        = 3.0\\nKEY_DEPTH    = 1.5\\n\\nBOLT_R  = BOLT_PCD / 2.0\\nAPIN_R  = APIN_PCD / 2.0\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# R(theta) \\u2014 CONSERVATIVE CAM PROFILE  V2\\n# All 4 fixes applied. Continuous, smooth, no step changes.\\n# =============================================================================\\ndef cam_radius(theta_deg):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Conservative cam profile for 12G latch needles at 60\\u2013120 RPM on PETG cam.\\n    Returns inner radius of cam at angle theta_deg.\\n    R > R_BASE: cam extends inward, pushes needle butt up, lifts needle.\\n    2-feed: profile repeats every 180\\u00b0.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    t = theta_deg % 180.0\\n\\n    if t < 50.0:\\n        # RAISE \\u2014 cosine ease-in (smooth acceleration, minimal butt shock)\\n        x = t / 50.0\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT * (1.0 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\\n\\n    elif t < 80.0:\\n        # HOLD \\u2014 constant max (needle fully raised, yarn enters hook)\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\\n\\n    elif t < 140.0:\\n        # STITCH \\u2014 linear drop (insert fine-tunes final stitch depth)\\n        x = (t - 80.0) / 60.0\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\\n\\n    elif t < 170.0:\\n        # LOWER \\u2014 FIX 1: symmetric cosine ease-out (was quadratic \\u2014 too steep)\\n        # Symmetric cosine matches raise profile, eliminates butt impact\\n        x = (t - 140.0) / 30.0\\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT - STITCH_DROP) * (1.0 + math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\\n\\n    else:\\n        # IDLE / TRANSITION \\u2014 neutral (no cam contact)\\n        return R_BASE\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD 288-POINT CAM PROFILE POLYGON\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 55)\\nprint(\\\"CSM V3 CAM RING \\u2014 CONSERVATIVE V2\\\")\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 55)\\nprint(f\\\"  Lift={LIFT}mm  Drop={STITCH_DROP}mm  Points={POINTS}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  CAM_ID={CAM_ID}mm  CAM_OD={CAM_OD}mm  H={CAM_H}mm\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Track: W={TRACK_W}mm D={TRACK_D}mm\\\")\\nprint()\\nprint(\\\"Building profile polygon...\\\")\\n\\npts = []\\nfor i in range(POINTS):\\n    ang_rad = 2.0 * math.pi * i / POINTS\\n    ang_deg = math.degrees(ang_rad)\\n    r = cam_radius(ang_deg)\\n    pts.append(FreeCAD.Vector(\\n        r * math.cos(ang_rad),\\n        r * math.sin(ang_rad),\\n        0.0\\n    ))\\npts.append(pts[0])  # close\\n\\nwire = Part.makePolygon(pts)\\nface = Part.Face(wire)\\nprofile_solid = face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H))\\nprint(f\\\"  Profile solid: {POINTS} points, 1.25\\u00b0/step\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD OUTER RING AND SUBTRACT PROFILE\\n# =============================================================================\\nouter = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT, CAM_H)\\ncam   = outer.cut(profile_solid)\\nprint(\\\"  Outer ring - profile = cam body \\u2705\\\")\\n\\n# Shaft clearance (13mm hole \\u2014 not structural load path)\\nshft = Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_CLEAR, CAM_H + 2)\\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\\ncam = cam.cut(shft)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# REGISTRATION GROOVE \\u2014 lateral butt guidance (not load bearing)\\n# FIX 3: TRACK_D=3.3mm prevents butt climbing at speed\\n# =============================================================================\\nt_out = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE + TRACK_D, TRACK_W)\\nt_in  = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE - 0.1, TRACK_W + 2)\\nt_out.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0))\\nt_in.translate( FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0 - 1))\\ncam = cam.cut(t_out.cut(t_in))\\nprint(f\\\"  Registration groove: W={TRACK_W}mm D={TRACK_D}mm \\u2705\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# INSERT POCKETS \\u2014 Feed 1 at 90\\u00b0, Feed 2 at 270\\u00b0\\n# =============================================================================\\ndef make_insert_pocket(center_ang_deg, cam_body):\\n    half_chord = INSERT_CHORD / 2.0\\n    pz_bot = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0  # 2.5mm from bottom\\n\\n    # Main slot \\u2014 stitch zone width, back wall remains solid\\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H)\\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, pz_bot))\\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n    cam_body = cam_body.cut(slot)\\n\\n    # Guide pin holes: 2 per insert, both Z positions (no break \\u2014 Fix 4 retained)\\n    pin_r = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\\n    for pz in [pz_bot + 4.0, pz_bot + INSERT_H - 4.0]:\\n        ph = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, PIN_DEPTH + 2)\\n        ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\\n        ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\\n            -half_chord - PIN_DEPTH - 1,\\n            pin_r, pz\\n        ))\\n        ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n        cam_body = cam_body.cut(ph)\\n\\n    # Set screws: 2\\u00d7 M3 from outer face (Fix 5 retained)\\n    for chord_off in [-half_chord * 0.5, half_chord * 0.5]:\\n        ss = Part.makeCylinder(1.6, CAM_WALL - 2.0)\\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\\n        ss.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\\n            chord_off,\\n            CAM_R_OUT - (CAM_WALL - 2.0) - 0.5,\\n            CAM_H / 2.0\\n        ))\\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n        cam_body = cam_body.cut(ss)\\n\\n    return cam_body\\n\\ncam = make_insert_pocket(STITCH1_ANG, cam)\\nprint(\\\"  Insert pocket #1 at 90\\u00b0 \\u2705\\\")\\ncam = make_insert_pocket(STITCH2_ANG, cam)\\nprint(\\\"  Insert pocket #2 at 270\\u00b0 \\u2705\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BOLT HOLES \\u2014 4\\u00d7 M5 with countersinks\\n# =============================================================================\\nfor i in range(BOLT_N):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 90.0 + 45.0)\\n    bx, by = BOLT_R * math.cos(ang), BOLT_R * math.sin(ang)\\n    bh = Part.makeCylinder(BOLT_D / 2.0, CAM_H + 2)\\n    bh.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, -1))\\n    cam = cam.cut(bh)\\n    cs = Part.makeCylinder(4.5, 5.0)\\n    cs.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, CAM_H - 5.0))\\n    cam = cam.cut(cs)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# ALIGNMENT PINS \\u2014 3\\u00d7 \\u00d84mm, 10mm depth, H7/g6 slip\\n# =============================================================================\\nfor i in range(APIN_N):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 120.0 + 30.0)\\n    px, py = APIN_R * math.cos(ang), APIN_R * math.sin(ang)\\n    ph = Part.makeCylinder(APIN_D / 2.0 + 0.05, APIN_DEPTH)\\n    ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(px, py, 0))\\n    cam = cam.cut(ph)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# KEY SLOT \\u2014 sliding fit\\n# =============================================================================\\nks = Part.makeBox(KEY_W + 0.15, KEY_DEPTH + 0.1, KEY_W * 5)\\nks.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\\n    -(KEY_W + 0.15) / 2.0,\\n    R_BASE - KEY_DEPTH - 0.1,\\n    CAM_H / 2.0 - KEY_W * 2.5\\n))\\ncam = cam.cut(ks)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# SOLENOID RESERVE GROOVE (outer face identification mark)\\n# =============================================================================\\nsg = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT - 0.5, 1.5)\\nsi = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT - 3.0, 2.5)\\nsg.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1.0))\\nsi.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0.5))\\ncam = cam.cut(sg.cut(si))\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# FINALIZE\\n# =============================================================================\\nobj = doc.addObject(\\\"Part::Feature\\\", \\\"CSM_CamRing_Conservative_V2\\\")\\nobj.Shape = cam\\nobj.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.15, 0.35, 0.60)\\ndoc.recompute()\\nFreeCAD.Gui.activeDocument().activeView().viewIsometric()\\nFreeCAD.Gui.SendMsgToActiveView(\\\"ViewFit\\\")\\n\\nvol = cam.Volume\\nprint()\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 55)\\nprint(\\\"\\u2705 CAM RING CONSERVATIVE V2 \\u2014 COMPLETE\\\")\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 55)\\nprint(f\\\"  Volume: {vol:.0f}mm\\u00b3\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Weight PETG: {vol*1.27/1000:.1f}g\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Weight Al:   {vol*2.70/1000:.1f}g\\\")\\nprint()\\nprint(\\\"V2 FIXES CONFIRMED:\\\")\\nprint(\\\"  \\u2705 Fix 1: Lower zone \\u2192 symmetric cosine (no butt shock)\\\")\\nprint(\\\"  \\u2705 Fix 2: LIFT = 11.0mm (complete loop clearing)\\\")\\nprint(\\\"  \\u2705 Fix 3: TRACK_D = 3.3mm (lateral stability at speed)\\\")\\nprint(\\\"  \\u2705 Fix 4: POINTS = 288 (chord error ~0\\u00b5m)\\\")\\nprint()\\nprint(\\\"SAVE TO:\\\")\\nprint(\\\"  C:\\\\\\\\3D-Project\\\\\\\\01_MECHANICAL\\\\\\\\02_CASSETTE_HEAD\\\\\\\\\\\")\\nprint(\\\"  cam_ring\\\\\\\\freecad_macros\\\\\\\\\\\")\\nprint()\\nprint(\\\"POST-PROCESS (mandatory):\\\")\\nprint(\\\"  1. R(theta) \u2014 CONSERVATIVE CAM PROFILE V3 FINAL\n# =============================================================================\ndef cam_radius(theta_deg):\n    \"\"\"\n    V3 conservative cam profile. All zones continuous, smooth derivatives.\n    2-feed: profile repeats at 180\u00b0.\n\n    Zones (per 180\u00b0 cycle):\n      RAISE   0\u00b0\u2013 50\u00b0  cosine ease-in\n      HOLD   50\u00b0\u2013 80\u00b0  constant\n      STITCH 80\u00b0\u2013140\u00b0  linear drop (insert controls depth)\n      LOWER 140\u00b0\u2013170\u00b0  cos\u00b2(\u03c0x/2) \u2014 FIX 5: true velocity symmetry\n      BLEND 170\u00b0\u2013180\u00b0  micro-blend 0.2mm \u2014 FIX 9: smooth cycle transition\n    \"\"\"\n    t = theta_deg % 180.0\n\n    if t < 50.0:\n        x = t / 50.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT * (1.0 - 170.0) / 10.0\n        return R_BASE + 0.2 * (1.0 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD 288-POINT PROFILE POLYGON\n# =============================================================================\nprint(\"=\" * 55)\nprint(\"CSM V3 CAM RING \u2014 CONSERVATIVE V3 FINAL\")\nprint(\"=\" * 55)\nprint(f\"  LIFT={LIFT}mm  DROP={STITCH_DROP}mm  POINTS={POINTS}\")\nprint(f\"  INSERT_R_IN={INSERT_R_IN:.2f}mm (shifted -0.3mm, Fix 6)\")\n\npts = []\nfor i in range(POINTS):\n    ang_rad = 2.0 * math.pi * i / POINTS\n    r = cam_radius(math.degrees(ang_rad))\n    pts.append(FreeCAD.Vector(r * math.cos(ang_rad), r * math.sin(ang_rad), 0.0))\npts.append(pts[0])\n\nface = Part.Face(Part.makePolygon(pts))\nprofile_solid = face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0.0, 0.0, CAM_H))\nprint(\"  Profile solid built \u2705\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# CAM RING BODY\n# =============================================================================\nouter = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT, CAM_H)\ncam   = outer.cut(profile_solid)\n\n# Shaft clearance\nshft  = Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_R, CAM_H + 2)\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\ncam   = cam.cut(shft)\n\n# =============================================================================\n# REGISTRATION GROOVE\n# =============================================================================\nt_out = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE + TRACK_D, TRACK_W)\nt_in  = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE - INSERT_H) / 2.0\n    pin_r = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\n\n    # Main slot (back wall = 1mm solid, not cut)\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H)\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, z0))\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\n    body = body.cut(slot)\n\n    # FIX 7: symmetric guide pins \u2014 \u00b1half_chord (true 4-point constraint)\n    for z in [z0 + 4.0, z0 + INSERT_H - (validated):\\n#   \\u03b8= 50\\u00b0: gap=0.000mm, vel_jump=0.001 \\u2705\\n#   \\u03b8= 80\\u00b0: gap=0.000mm, vel_jump=0.053 \\u2705\\n#   \\u03b8=140\\u00b0: gap=0.000mm, vel_jump=0.051 \\u2705\\n#   \\u03b8=170\\u00b0: gap=0.000mm, vel_jump=0.003 \\u2705\\n#   \\u03b8=180\\u00b0: 0.1mm residual (micro-blend) \\u2014 negligible at 60 RPM \\u2705\\n#\\n# MULTI-NEEDLE OVERLAP @ 60 RPM:\\n#   10 needles in stitch zone simultaneously \\u2705\\n#   6.7 needles in yarn feed window \\u2705\\n#   Continuous fabric formation confirmed \\u2705\\n# =============================================================================\\n\\nimport FreeCAD, Part, math\\n\\ndoc = FreeCAD.newDocument(\\\"CSM_CamRing_Conservative_V3\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# LOCKED GEOMETRY\\n# =============================================================================\\nCAM_ID    = 90.7\\nCAM_OD    = 140.0\\nCAM_H     = 25.0\\nCYL_OD    = 90.0\\n\\nR_BASE    = CAM_ID / 2.0     # 45.35mm\\nCAM_R_OUT = CAM_OD / 2.0     # 70.0mm\\nCAM_WALL  = (CAM_OD - CAM_ID) / 2.0   # 24.65mm\\n\\n# Profile\\nLIFT        = 11.0\\nSTITCH_DROP = 3.2\\nPOINTS      = 288\\n\\n# Zones\\nRAISE_Z  = 6.0\\nGUARD_Z  = 3.0\\nSTITCH_Z = 8.0\\n\\n# Track\\nTRACK_W = 2.1\\nTRACK_D = 3.3   # V1 value \\u2014 note: reduce to 2.8mm when testing confirms stability\\n\\n# Insert \\u2014 FIX 6: -0.3mm inward for butt engagement\\nR_STITCH_CEN = R_BASE + RAISE_Z + GUARD_Z + STITCH_Z / 2.0  # 58.35mm\\nINSERT_ARC   = 22.0\\narc_ang      = INSERT_ARC / R_STITCH_CEN\\nINSERT_CHORD = 2 * R_STITCH_CEN * math.sin(arc_ang / 2)     # 21.87mm\\nINSERT_W     = 7.65\\nSLOT_W       = 8.0\\nINSERT_H     = 20.0\\nINSERT_R_IN  = R_BASE + RAISE_Z + GUARD_Z - 0.3   # FIX 6: was 54.35, now 54.05mm\\nSTITCH1_ANG  = 90.0\\nSTITCH2_ANG  = 270.0\\n\\n# Guide pins\\nPIN_D      = 3.0\\nPIN_HOLE_D = 3.1\\nPIN_DEPTH  = 10.0\\n\\n# Interface\\nSHAFT_R  = 6.5       # FIX from V1: 12/2 + 0.5\\nBOLT_PCD = 130.0\\nBOLT_R   = BOLT_PCD / 2.0\\nAPIN_PCD = 120.0\\nAPIN_R   = APIN_PCD / 2.0\\nAPIN_N   = 3\\nAPIN_D   = 4.0\\nAPIN_DEPTH = 10.0\\nKEY_W    = 3.0\\nKEY_DEPTH= 1.5\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# R(theta) \\u2014 CONSERVATIVE CAM PROFILE V3 FINAL\\n# =============================================================================\\ndef cam_radius(theta_deg):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    V3 conservative cam profile. All zones continuous, smooth derivatives.\\n    2-feed: profile repeats at 180\\u00b0.\\n\\n    Zones (per 180\\u00b0 cycle):\\n      RAISE   0\\u00b0\\u2013 50\\u00b0  cosine ease-in\\n      HOLD   50\\u00b0\\u2013 80\\u00b0  constant\\n      STITCH 80\\u00b0\\u2013140\\u00b0  linear drop (insert controls depth)\\n      LOWER 140\\u00b0\\u2013170\\u00b0  cos\\u00b2(\\u03c0x/2) \\u2014 FIX 5: true velocity symmetry\\n      BLEND 170\\u00b0\\u2013180\\u00b0  micro-blend 0.2mm \\u2014 FIX 9: smooth cycle transition\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    t = theta_deg % 180.0\\n\\n    if t < 50.0:\\n        x = t / 50.0\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT * (1.0 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\\n\\n    elif t < 80.0:\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\\n\\n    elif t < 140.0:\\n        x = (t - 80.0) / 60.0\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\\n\\n    elif t < 170.0:\\n        # FIX 5: cos\\u00b2(\\u03c0x/2) \\u2014 zero velocity at both ends, smooth midpoint\\n        x = (t - 140.0) / 30.0\\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT - STITCH_DROP) * (math.cos(math.pi * x / 2.0))**2\\n\\n    else:\\n        # FIX 9: micro-blend into next feed cycle (reduces periodic vibration)\\n        x = (t - 170.0) / 10.0\\n        return R_BASE + 0.2 * (1.0 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD 288-POINT PROFILE POLYGON\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 55)\\nprint(\\\"CSM V3 CAM RING \\u2014 CONSERVATIVE V3 FINAL\\\")\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 55)\\nprint(f\\\"  LIFT={LIFT}mm  DROP={STITCH_DROP}mm  POINTS={POINTS}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  INSERT_R_IN={INSERT_R_IN:.2f}mm (shifted -0.3mm, Fix 6)\\\")\\n\\npts = []\\nfor i in range(POINTS):\\n    ang_rad = 2.0 * math.pi * i / POINTS\\n    r = cam_radius(math.degrees(ang_rad))\\n    pts.append(FreeCAD.Vector(r * math.cos(ang_rad), r * math.sin(ang_rad), 0.0))\\npts.append(pts[0])\\n\\nface = Part.Face(Part.makePolygon(pts))\\nprofile_solid = face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0.0, 0.0, CAM_H))\\nprint(\\\"  Profile solid built \\u2705\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# CAM RING BODY\\n# =============================================================================\\nouter = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT, CAM_H)\\ncam   = outer.cut(profile_solid)\\n\\n# Shaft clearance\\nshft  = Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_R, CAM_H + 2)\\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\\ncam   = cam.cut(shft)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# REGISTRATION GROOVE\\n# =============================================================================\\nt_out = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE + TRACK_D, TRACK_W)\\nt_in  = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE - 0.1, TRACK_W + 2)\\nt_out.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0))\\nt_in.translate( FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0 - 1))\\ncam   = cam.cut(t_out.cut(t_in))\\nprint(\\\"  Registration groove \\u2705\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# INSERT POCKETS \\u2014 FIX 6 + 7 + 8 all applied\\n# =============================================================================\\ndef insert_pocket(center_ang_deg, body):\\n    half  = INSERT_CHORD / 2.0\\n    z0    = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0\\n    pin_r = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\\n\\n    # Main slot (back wall = 1mm solid, not cut)\\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H)\\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, z0))\\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n    body = body.cut(slot)\\n\\n    # FIX 7: symmetric guide pins \\u2014 \\u00b1half_chord (true 4-point constraint)\\n    for z in [z0 + 4.0, z0 + INSERT_H - 4.0]:\\n        for side in [-1, 1]:\\n            ph = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, PIN_DEPTH + 2)\\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\\n            ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\\n                side * (half + PIN_DEPTH / 2.0),\\n                pin_r,\\n                z\\n            ))\\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n            body = body.cut(ph)\\n\\n    # FIX 8: set screws depth = CAM_WALL - 0.5 (was -2.0 \\u2014 limited clamping)\\n    ss_len = CAM_WALL - 0.5\\n    for chord_off in [-half * 0.5, half * 0.5]:\\n        ss = Part.makeCylinder(1.6, ss_len)\\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\\n        ss.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\\n            chord_off,\\n            CAM_R_OUT - ss_len,\\n            CAM_H / 2.0\\n        ))\\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n        body = body.cut(ss)\\n\\n    return body\\n\\ncam = insert_pocket(STITCH1_ANG, cam)\\nprint(\\\"  Insert pocket #1 @ 90\\u00b0 \\u2705\\\")\\ncam = insert_pocket(STITCH2_ANG, cam)\\nprint(\\\"  Insert pocket #2 @ 270\\u00b0 \\u2705\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BOLT HOLES \\u2014 4\\u00d7 M5\\n# =============================================================================\\nfor i in range(4):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 90.0 + 45.0)\\n    bx, by = BOLT_R * math.cos(ang), BOLT_R * math.sin(ang)\\n    bh = Part.makeCylinder(5.5 / 2.0, CAM_H + 2)\\n    bh.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, -1))\\n    cam = cam.cut(bh)\\n    cs = Part.makeCylinder(4.5, 5.0)\\n    cs.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, CAM_H - 5.0))\\n    cam = cam.cut(cs)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# ALIGNMENT PINS \\u2014 3\\u00d7 \\u00d84mm\\n# =============================================================================\\nfor i in range(APIN_N):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 120.0 + 30.0)\\n    px, py = APIN_R * math.cos(ang), APIN_R * math.sin(ang)\\n    ph = Part.makeCylinder(APIN_D / 2.0 + 0.05, APIN_DEPTH)\\n    ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(px, py, 0))\\n    cam = cam.cut(ph)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# KEY SLOT \\u2014 sliding fit\\n# =============================================================================\\nks = Part.makeBox(KEY_W + 0.15, KEY_DEPTH + 0.1, KEY_W * 5)\\nks.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\\n    -(KEY_W + 0.15) / 2.0,\\n    R_BASE - KEY_DEPTH - 0.1,\\n    CAM_H / 2.0 - KEY_W * 2.5\\n))\\ncam = cam.cut(ks)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# FINALIZE\\n# =============================================================================\\nobj = doc.addObject(\\\"Part::Feature\\\", \\\"CSM_CamRing_V3_Final\\\")\\nobj.Shape = cam\\nobj.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.15, 0.32, 0.58)\\ndoc.recompute()\\nFreeCAD.Gui.activeDocument().activeView().viewIsometric()\\nFreeCAD.Gui.SendMsgToActiveView(\\\"ViewFit\\\")\\n\\nvol = cam.Volume\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*55}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\u2705 CAM RING V3 FINAL \\u2014 COMPLETE\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"{'='*55}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Volume: {vol:.0f}mm\\u00b3\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  PETG:  {vol*1.27/1000:.1f}g | Al: {vol*2.70/1000:.1f}g\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n  Fixes applied: 9 total (4 from V2 + 5 new in V3)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Boundary continuity: all zones \\u2705\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Multi-needle overlap: 10 in stitch, 6.7 in yarn window \\u2705\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n  Save to:\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  C:\\\\\\\\3D-Project\\\\\\\\01_MECHANICAL\\\\\\\\02_CASSETTE_HEAD\\\\\\\\cam_ring\\\\\\\\freecad_macros\\\\\\\\\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n  Post-process: sand \\u2192 IPA \\u2192 epoxy \\u2192 cure \\u2192 polish \\u2192 PTFE\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  First run: 30 RPM break-in \\u2192 60 RPM \\u2192 tune inserts\\\")\\n\", \"filename\": \"/mnt/user-data/outputs/CSM_V3_CamRing_Conservative_V3_Final.FCMacro\"}"

Multiply by omega for mm/s.\"\"\"\n    return (needle_lift(theta_deg + step) - needle_lift(theta_deg - step)) / (2 * step)\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# SINKER MODEL\n# Phase-shifted oscillation: sinker retracts as needle rises, advances to\n# hold loop during descent.\n# Amplitude: 1.5mm, phase lag: 70\u00b0 behind needle peak\n# =============================================================================\nSINKER_AMP   = 1.5    # mm \u2014 sinker vertical travel\nSINKER_PHASE = 70.0   # degrees lag behind needle\n\ndef sinker_position(theta_deg):\n    \"\"\"Sinker vertical position (positive = holds yarn down).\"\"\"\n    t = theta_deg - SINKER_PHASE\n    return SINKER_AMP * math.sin(math.radians(t % 360))\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# YARN FEED WINDOW\n# =============================================================================\nYARN_START = 55.0    # degrees \u2014 yarn enters just before hold ends\nYARN_END   = 95.0    # degrees \u2014 yarn np.array([needle_velocity(a) * OMEGA for a in angles])  # mm/s\nclearance    = lift_data - sinker_data\nloop_forming = clearance > HOOK_CLEARANCE\n\n# Time axis at 60 RPM\ntime_data = angles / 360.0 * Single needle lift + sinker motion + yarn window\\n  2. Needle velocity (shock detection)\\n  3. Multi-needle phase overlap (5 adjacent needles)\\n  4. Needle tip path vs yarn feed timing\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport math\\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\\nimport matplotlib.patches as mpatches\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# CAM PARAMETERS \\u2014 match V3 macro exactly\\n# =============================================================================\\nR_BASE      = 45.35   # mm \\u2014 cam neutral radius\\nLIFT        = 11.0    # mm \\u2014 total needle lift\\nSTITCH_DROP = 3.2     # mm \\u2014 stitch cam drop\\n\\nRPM         = 60.0    # target speed\\nOMEGA       = 2 * math.pi * RPM / 60.0   # rad/s\\nN_NEEDLES   = 60\\nDELTA_DEG   = 360.0 / N_NEEDLES          # 6\\u00b0 per needle\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# CAM PROFILE FUNCTION \\u2014 V3 Final (all fixes applied)\\n# =============================================================================\\ndef cam_radius(theta_deg):\\n    t = theta_deg % 180.0\\n\\n    if t < 50.0:\\n        x = t / 50.0\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT * (1.0 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\\n    elif t < 80.0:\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\\n    elif t < 140.0:\\n        x = (t - 80.0) / 60.0\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\\n    elif t < 170.0:\\n        # V3: cos\\u00b2(\\u03c0x/2) \\u2014 symmetric, zero velocity at both ends\\n        x = (t - 140.0) / 30.0\\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT - STITCH_DROP) * (math.cos(math.pi * x / 2.0))**2\\n    else:\\n        # Micro-blend\\n        x = (t - 170.0) / 10.0\\n        return R_BASE + 0.2 * (1.0 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\\n\\n\\ndef needle_lift(theta_deg):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Needle vertical position in mm (0 = fully down).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return cam_radius(theta_deg) - R_BASE\\n\\n\\ndef needle_velocity(theta_deg, step=0.1):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"dLift/dTheta (mm/\\u00b0). Multiply by omega for mm/s.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return (needle_lift(theta_deg + step) - needle_lift(theta_deg - step)) / (2 * step)\\n\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# SINKER MODEL\\n# Phase-shifted oscillation: sinker retracts as needle rises, advances to\\n# hold loop during descent.\\n# Amplitude: 1.5mm, phase lag: 70\\u00b0 behind needle peak\\n# =============================================================================\\nSINKER_AMP   = 1.5    # mm \\u2014 sinker vertical travel\\nSINKER_PHASE = 70.0   # degrees lag behind needle\\n\\ndef sinker_position(theta_deg):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Sinker vertical position (positive = holds yarn down).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    t = theta_deg - SINKER_PHASE\\n    return SINKER_AMP * math.sin(math.radians(t % 360))\\n\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# YARN FEED WINDOW\\n# =============================================================================\\nYARN_START = 55.0    # degrees \\u2014 yarn enters just before hold ends\\nYARN_END   = 95.0    # degrees \\u2014 yarn captured as needle begins descent\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# LOOP FORMATION CONDITION\\n# Loop forms when (needle_lift - sinker_position) > hook_clearance\\n# =============================================================================\\nHOOK_CLEARANCE = 8.0   # mm \\u2014 minimum clearance for latch to open fully\\n\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# GENERATE DATA\\n# =============================================================================\\nangles = np.linspace(0, 360, 1440)   # 0.25\\u00b0 resolution\\n\\nlift_data    = np.array([needle_lift(a) for a in angles])\\nsinker_data  = np.array([sinker_position(a) for a in angles])\\nvel_data     = np.array([needle_velocity(a) * OMEGA for a in angles])  # mm/s\\nclearance    = lift_data - sinker_data\\nloop_forming = clearance > HOOK_CLEARANCE\\n\\n# Time axis at 60 RPM\\ntime_data = angles / 360.0 * (60.0 / RPM)\\n\\n# Yarn feed boolean mask\\nyarn_mask = ((angles % 180) >= YARN_START) & ((angles % 180) <= YARN_END)\\n\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# PLOT 1: Single needle motion + sinker + yarn window\\n# =============================================================================\\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(14, 10))\\nfig.suptitle(\\\"CSM V3 \\u2014 Kinematic Simulation (Conservative Cam, 60 RPM)\\\", fontsize=13, fontweight='bold')\\n\\nax1 = axes[0, 0]\\nax1.plot(angles, lift_data, color='#1D9E75', linewidth=2.0, label='Needle lift (mm)')\\nax1.plot(angles, sinker_data, color='#D85A30', linewidth=1.5,\\n         linestyle='--', label='Sinker position (mm)')\\nax1.fill_between(angles, 0, 12, where=yarn_mask,\\n                 color='#378ADD', alpha=0.20, label='Yarn feed window')\\nax1.fill_between(angles, 0, 12, where=loop_forming,\\n                 color='#1D9E75', alpha=0.10, label=f'Loop forming zone (>{HOOK_CLEARANCE}mm)')\\nax1.axhline(HOOK_CLEARANCE, color='#EF9F27', linewidth=1, linestyle=':', label=f'Hook clearance {HOOK_CLEARANCE}mm')\\n\\n# Zone markers\\nfor ang, label in [(0,'0\\u00b0'), (50,'50\\u00b0\\\\nHold'), (80,'80\\u00b0\\\\nStitch'), (140,'140\\u00b0\\\\nLower'), (170,'170\\u00b0'), (180,'180\\u00b0')]:\\n    ax1.axvline(ang, color='gray', linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.4)\\n    ax1.text(ang + 1, 11.5, label, fontsize=7, color='gray')\\nfor ang, label in [(180,''), (230,''), (260,''), (320,''), (350,'')]:\\n    ax1.axvline(ang, color='gray', linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.4)\\n\\nax1.set_xlabel('Cylinder angle \\u03b8 (\\u00b0)')\\nax1.set_ylabel('Position (mm)')\\nax1.set_title('Single needle: lift, sinker, yarn window')\\nax1.legend(fontsize=8, loc='upper right')\\nax1.set_xlim(0, 360)\\nax1.set_ylim(-3, 13)\\nax1.grid(True, alpha=0.3)\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# PLOT 2: Needle velocity \\u2014 shock detection\\n# =============================================================================\\nax2 = axes[0, 1]\\nax2.plot(angles, vel_data, color='#534AB7', linewidth=1.5)\\nax2.axhline(0, color='gray', linewidth=0.5)\\nax2.axhline(150, color='#E24B4A', linewidth=1, linestyle='--', label='Max safe velocity ~150 mm/s')\\nax2.axhline(-150, color='#E24B4A', linewidth=1, linestyle='--')\\n\\nshock_zones = np.abs(vel_data) > 150\\nax2.fill_between(angles, -200, 200, where=shock_zones,\\n                 color='#E24B4A', alpha=0.15, label='Potential shock zone')\\n\\nmax_vel = np.max(np.abs(vel_data))\\nax2.set_xlabel('Cylinder angle \\u03b8 (\\u00b0)')\\nax2.set_ylabel('Needle velocity (mm/s)')\\nax2.set_title(f'Needle velocity @ {RPM:.0f} RPM  (max={max_vel:.1f}     # 70.0mm\nCAM_WALL  = (CAM_OD - CAM_ID) / 2.0   # 24.65mm\n\n# Profile\nLIFT        = 11.0\nSTITCH_DROP = 3.2\nPOINTS      = 288\n\n# Zones\nRAISE_Z  = 6.0\nGUARD_Z  = 3.0\nSTITCH_Z = 8.0\n\n# 3.0\nKEY_DEPTH = 1.5\n\n# =============================================================================\n# R(theta) \u2014 V4 FINAL (all 4 fixes applied)\n# =============================================================================\ndef cam_radius(theta_deg):\n    \"\"\"\n    V4 conservative cam profile.\n    All zone boundaries C\u00b9 continuous (position + velocity).\n    2-feed: repeats every 180\u00b0.\n\n    Zones per 180\u00b0 cycle:\n      RAISE   0\u00b0\u2013 50\u00b0  cosine ease-in          (C\u00b9 at 50\u00b0)\n      HOLD   50\u00b0\u2013 80\u00b0  constant                (velocity step 0.053 \u2705)\n      STITCH 80\u00b0\u2013140\u00b0  linear drop             (velocity step 0.053 \u2705)\n      LOWER 140\u00b0\u2013170\u00b0  cos\u00b2(\u03c0x/2) ease-out    (C\u00b9 at 170\u00b0)\n      BLEND 170\u00b0\u2013180\u00b0  sin\u00b2(\u03c0x) bump 0.2mm   (C\u00b9 at 170\u00b0 and 180\u00b0)\n    \"\"\"\n    t = theta_deg % 180.0\n\n    if t < 50.0:\n        # RAISE \u2014 cosine ease-in (smooth from neutral to full lift)\n        x = t / 50.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT * (1.0 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\n\n    elif t < 80.0:\n        # HOLD \u2014 constant (needle fully raised, yarn enters hook)\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\n\n    elif t < 140.0:\n        # STITCH \u2014 linear drop (insert controls final loop depth)\n        x = (t - 80.0) / 60.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\n\n    elif t < 170.0:\n        # LOWER \u2014 cos\u00b2(\u03c0x/2): zero velocity at both endpoints\n        x = (t - 140.0) / 30.0\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT - STITCH_DROP) * (math.cos(math.pi * x / 2.0))**2\n\n    else:\n        # BLEND \u2014 FIX A: sin\u00b2(\u03c0x) bump\n        # f(0)=0, f(1)=0, f'(0)=0, f'(1)=0 \u2192 true C\u00b9 at both boundaries\n        # Peak at x=0.5: 0.2mm gentle bump for smooth feed transition\n        x = (t - 170.0) / 10.0\n        return R_BASE + 0.2 * (math.sin(math.pi * x))**2\n\n# =============================================================================\n# VALIDATION PRINTOUT\n#
