testing)\")\nprint()\n\neps = 0.001\nprint(\"Boundary continuity:\")\nfor b in [50, 80, 140, 170, 180]:\n    r = cam_radius(b)\n    vb = (cam_radius(b) - cam_radius(b - eps)) / eps\n    va = (cam_radius(b + eps) - cam_radius(b)) / eps\n    vj = abs(va - vb)\n    status = \"OK\" if vj < 0.1 else \"WARN\"\n    print(f\"  [{status}] theta={b:3d}  R={r:.4f}mm  vel_jump={vj:.4f}\")\n\nprint()\nkey_pts = [(0,'RAISE start'), (25,'raise mid'), (50,'HOLD peak'),\n           (80,'STITCH start'), (110,'stitch mid'), (140,'LOWER start'),\n           (155,'lower POINTS\n    r = cam_radius(math.degrees(ang_rad))\n    pts.append(FreeCAD.Vector(r * math.cos(ang_rad), r * math.sin(ang_rad), 0.0))\npts.append(pts[0])\n\nface    = Part.Face(Part.makePolygon(pts))\nprofile = face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0.0, 0.0, CAM_H))\n\nouter = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT, CAM_H)\ncam   = outer.cut(profile)\n\nshft  = Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_R, CAM_H + 2)\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\ncam   = cam.cut(shft)\nprint(\"  Body + profile + shaft \u2705\")\n\n# FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0 - 1))\ncam   = cam.cut(t_out.cut(t_in))\nprint(f\"  Groove: W={TRACK_W}mm D={TRACK_D}mm \u2705\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# INSERT POCKETS \u2014 all V3+V4 fixes applied\n# =============================================================================\ndef insert_pocket(center_ang_deg, body):\n    half  = INSERT_CHORD / 2.0\n    z0    = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0\n    pin_r = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\n\n    # Main slot\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H)\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, z0))\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\n    body = body.cut(slot)\n\n    # Symmetric guide pins \u00b1half_chord (V3 fix \u2014 4-point constraint)\n    for z in [z0 + 4.0, z0 + INSERT_H - 4.0]:\n        for side in [-1, 1]:\n            ph = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, PIN_DEPTH + 2)\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), [-half * 0.5, half * 0.5]:\n        ss = Part.makeCylinder(1.6, ss_len)\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\n        ss.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\n return body\n\ncam = insert_pocket(STITCH1_ANG, cam)\nprint(\"  Insert #1 @ 90\u00b0 \u2705\")\ncam = insert_pocket(STITCH2_ANG, cam)\nprint(\"  Insert #2 @ 270\u00b0 \u2705\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BOLTS, PINS, KEY\n# =============================================================================\nfor i in range(4):\n    ang = math.radians(i * 90.0 + 45.0)\n    bx, by = BOLT_R * math.cos(ang), BOLT_R * math.sin(ang)\n    bh = Part.makeCylinder(5.5 / 2.0, CAM_H + 2)\n    bh.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, -1))\n    cam = cam.cut(bh)\n    cs = Part.makeCylinder(4.5, 5.0)\n    cs.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, CAM_H - 5.0))\n    cam = cam.cut(cs)\n\nfor i in range(APIN_N):\n    ang = math.radians(i * 120.0 +  -0.5mm left only 0.5mm inner wall \\u2192 cracking risk in PETG\\n#     -1.0mm leaves 1.0mm inner wall \\u2192 safe clamping force\\n#\\n#   FIX D \\u2014 TRACK_D comment clarified: keep 3.3mm for     = 3.0\\nPIN_HOLE_D   = 3.1\\nPIN_DEPTH    = 10.0\\n\\n# Interface\\nSHAFT_R   = 6.5\\nBOLT_PCD  = 130.0\\nBOLT_R    = BOLT_PCD / 2.0\\nAPIN_PCD  = 120.0\\nAPIN_R    = APIN_PCD / 2.0\\nAPIN_N    = / 50.0\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT * (1.0 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\\n\\n    elif t < 80.0:\\n        # HOLD \\u2014 constant (needle fully raised, yarn enters hook)\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\\n\\n    elif t < 140.0:\\n        # STITCH \\u2014 linear drop (insert controls final loop depth)\\n        x = (t - 80.0) / 60.0\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\\n\\n    elif t 0.2mm gentle bump for smooth feed transition\\n        x = (t - 170.0) / 10.0\\n        return R_BASE + 0.2 * (math.sin(math.pi * x))**2\\n\\n# CAM_ID) / 2.0   # 24.65mm\n\n# Profile\nLIFT        = 11.0   # mm \u2014 total needle lift (12G validated)\nSTITCH_DROP = 3.2    # mm \u2014 nominal; insert adjusts \u00b11.5mm\nPOINTS      = 288    # polygon resolution (1.25\u00b0/step)\n\n# Zones\nRAISE_Z  = 6.0\nGUARD_Z  = 3.0\nSTITCH_Z = 8.0\n\n# Track\nTRACK_W = 2.1    # mm \u2014 butt width + 0.10mm total clearance\nTRACK_D = 3.3    # mm \u2014 keep for ALL V1 testing, do not reduce\n\n# Insert geometry \u2014 corrected coordinate system\nR_STITCH_CEN = R_BASE + RAISE_Z + GUARD_Z + STITCH_Z / 2.0   # 58.35mm\nINSERT_ARC   = 22.0   # mm arc length at R_STITCH_CEN\narc_ang      = INSERT_ARC / R_STITCH_CEN\nINSERT_CHORD = 2 * R_STITCH_CEN * math.sin(arc_ang / 2)       # 21.87mm\nINSERT_W     = 7.65   # mm \u2014 insert body\nSLOT_W       = 8.0    # mm \u2014 pocket (0.175mm clearance per side)\nINSERT_H     = 20.0   # mm \u2014 axial height\nINSERT_R_IN  = R_BASE + RAISE_Z + GUARD_Z - 0.15   # 54.20mm (-0.15mm for engagement)\nSTITCH1_ANG  = 90.0\nSTITCH2_ANG  = 270.0\n\n# Guide pins\nPIN_D      = 3.0\nPIN_HOLE_D = 3.1    # H7/g6 slip\nPIN_DEPTH  = 10.0\n\n# Interface\nSHAFT_R   = 6.5     # 12mm/2 + 0.5mm clearance\nBOLT_PCD  = 130.0\nBOLT_R    = BOLT_PCD / 2.0\nAPIN_PCD  = 120.0\nAPIN_R    = APIN_PCD / 2.0\nAPIN_N    = 3\nAPIN_D    = 4.0\nAPIN_DEPTH= 10.0\nKEY_W     = 3.0\nKEY_DEPTH = 1.5\n\n# =============================================================================\n# R(theta) \u2014 V5 FINAL\n# =============================================================================\ndef cam_radius(theta_deg):\n    \"\"\"\n    Print-ready conservative cam profile for 12G latch needles.\n    PETG cam, 60-120 RPM target range.\n    All zone boundaries C\u00b9 continuous.\n    2-feed architecture: profile repeats every 180\u00b0.\n    \"\"\"\n    t = theta_deg % 180.0\n\n    if t < 50.0:\n        # RAISE \u2014 cosine ease-in\n        x = t / 50.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT * (1.0 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\n\n    elif t < 80.0:\n        # HOLD \u2014 constant (yarn capture window)\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\n\n    elif t < 140.0:\n        # STITCH \u2014 linear (insert fine-tunes depth \u00b11.5mm)\n        x = (t - 80.0) / 60.0\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\n\n    elif t < 170.0:\n        # LOWER \u2014 cos\u00b2(\u03c0x/2): zero derivative at both ends\n        x = (t - 140.0) / 30.0\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT - STITCH_DROP) * (math.cos(math.pi * x / 2.0))**2\n\n    else:\n        # BLEND \u2014 FIX A: 0.1 * sin\u00b2(\u03c0x)\n        # f(0)=0, f(1)=0, f'(0)=0, f'(1)=0 \u2192 C\u00b9 at 170\u00b0 and 180\u00b0\n        # Peak = 0.1mm at x=0.5 (negligible needle motion)\n        x = (t - 170.0) / 10.0\n        return R_BASE + 0.1 * (math.sin(math.pi * x))**2\n\n# =============================================================================\n# VALIDATION\n# =============================================================================\nprint(\"=\" * 58)\nprint(\"CSM V3 CAM RING \u2014 V5 PRINT-READY PROTOTYPE\")\nprint(\"=\" * 58)\nprint(f\"  LIFT={LIFT}mm  DROP={STITCH_DROP}mm  POINTS={POINTS}\")\nprint(f\"  INSERT_R_IN={INSERT_R_IN:.2f}mm  TRACK_D={TRACK_D}mm\")\nprint()\n\neps = 0.001\nprint(\"Boundary continuity:\")\nfor b, zone in [(50,'raise\u2192hold'),(80,'hold\u2192stitch'),(140,'stitch\u2192lower'),(170,'lower\u2192blend'),(180,'blend\u2192raise2')]:\n    r  = cam_radius(b)\n    vb = (cam_radius(b) - cam_radius(b - eps)) / eps\n    va = (cam_radius(b + eps) - cam_radius(b)) / eps\n    vj = abs(va - vb)\n    sym = \"OK\" if vj < 0.1 else \"WARN\"\n    print(f\"  [{sym}] {b:3d}\u00b0 R={r:.4f}mm  vel_jump={vj:.4f}  [{zone}]\")\n\nprint()\nprint(\"Profile (key angles, one 180\u00b0 cycle):\")\nfor ang, lbl in [(0,'RAISE start'),(25,'raise mid'),(50,'HOLD peak'),\n                  (80,'STITCH start'),(110,'stitch mid'),(140,'LOWER start'),\n                  (155,'lower mid'),(170,'BLEND start'),(175,'blend peak'),\n                  (179,'blend end'),(180,'next cycle start')]:\n    r = cam_radius(ang)\n    print(f\"  {ang:3d}\u00b0  lift={r-R_BASE:.3f}mm  [{lbl}]\")\n\nprint()\nprint(\"V5 fixes applied:\")\nprint(\"  A: blend 0.1*sin^2 (peak=0.1mm, was 0.2mm)\")\nprint(\"  B: pin offset side*(half+2.0) (centered on insert body)\")\nprint(\"  C: set screw wall 1.5mm (was 1.0mm, crack risk)\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD\n# =============================================================================\nprint(\"\\nBuilding geometry...\")\npts = []\nfor i in range(POINTS):\n    a = 2.0 * math.pi * i / POINTS\n    r = cam_radius(math.degrees(a))\n    pts.append(FreeCAD.Vector(r * math.cos(a), r * math.sin(a), 0.0))\npts.append(pts[0])\n\nface    = Part.Face(Part.makePolygon(pts))\nprofile = face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H))\n\nouter = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT, CAM_H)\ncam   = outer.cut(profile)\n\nshft = Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_R, CAM_H + 2)\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\ncam  = cam.cut(shft)\n\n# Registration groove\nt_out = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE + TRACK_D, TRACK_W)\nt_in  = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE - 0.1, TRACK_W + 2)\nt_out.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0))\nt_in.translate( FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0 - 1))\ncam  = cam.cut(t_out.cut(t_in))\nprint(f\"  Ring + profile + groove \u2705\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# INSERT POCKETS \u2014 all V5 fixes applied\n# =============================================================================\ndef insert_pocket(center_ang_deg, body):\n    half  = INSERT_CHORD / 2.0\n    z0    = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0\n    pin_r = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\n\n    # Main slot\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H)\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, z0))\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\n    body = body.cut(slot)\n\n    # FIX B: guide pins at side*(half+2.0) \u2014 centered on insert body\n    for z in [z0 + 4.0, z0 + INSERT_H - 4.0]:\n        for side in [-1, 1]:\n            ph = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, PIN_DEPTH + 2)\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\n            ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\n                side * (half + 2.0),   # FIX B: was half+PIN_DEPTH/2\n                pin_r,\n                z\n            ))\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\n            body = body.cut(ph)\n\n    # FIX C: set screws with 1.5mm inner wall\n    ss_len = CAM_WALL - 1.5   # FIX C: was -1.0\n    for chord_off in [-half * 0.5, half * 0.5]:\n        ss = Part.makeCylinder(1.6, ss_len)\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\n        ss.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(\n            chord_off,\n            CAM_R_OUT - ss_len,\n            CAM_H / 2.0\n        ))\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\n        body = body.cut(ss)\n\n    return body\n\ncam = insert_pocket(STITCH1_ANG, cam)\ncam = insert_pocket(STITCH2_ANG, cam)\nprint(\"  Insert pockets #1 (90\u00b0) + #2 (270\u00b0) \u2705\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BOLTS \u2014 4\u00d7 M5\n# =============================================================================\nfor i in range(4):\n    ang = math.radians(i * 90.0 + 45.0)\n    bx, by = BOLT_R * math.cos(ang), BOLT_R * math.sin(ang)\n    bh = Part.makeCylinder(5.5 / 2.0, CAM_H + 2)\n    bh.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, -1))\n    cam = cam.cut(bh)\n    cs = Part.makeCylinder(4.5, 5.0)\n    cs.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, CAM_H - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\\n\\n    elif t < 80.0:\\n        # HOLD \\u2014 constant (yarn capture window)\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\\n\\n    elif t < 140.0:\\n        # STITCH \\u2014 linear (insert fine-tunes depth \\u00b11.5mm)\\n        x = (t - 80.0) / 60.0\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\\n\\n    elif t < 170.0:\\n        # LOWER \\u2014 cos\\u00b2(\\u03c0x/2): zero derivative at both ends\\n        x = (t - 140.0) / 30.0\\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT - STITCH_DROP) * (math.cos(math.pi * x / 2.0))**2\\n\\n    else:\\n        # BLEND \\u2014 FIX A: 0.1 * sin\\u00b2(\\u03c0x)\\n        # f(0)=0, f(1)=0, f'(0)=0, f'(1)=0 \\u2192 C\\u00b9 at 170\\u00b0 and 180\\u00b0\\n        # Peak = 0.1mm at x=0.5 (negligible needle motion)\\n        x = (t - 170.0) / 10.0\\n        return R_BASE + 0.1 * (math.sin(math.pi * x))**2\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# VALIDATION\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 58)\\nprint(\\\"CSM V3 CAM RING \\u2014 V5 PRINT-READY PROTOTYPE\\\")\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 58)\\nprint(f\\\"  LIFT={LIFT}mm  DROP={STITCH_DROP}mm  POINTS={POINTS}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  INSERT_R_IN={INSERT_R_IN:.2f}mm  TRACK_D={TRACK_D}mm\\\")\\nprint()\\n\\neps = 0.001\\nprint(\\\"Boundary continuity:\\\")\\nfor b, zone in [(50,'raise\\u2192hold'),(80,'hold\\u2192stitch'),(140,'stitch\\u2192lower'),(170,'lower\\u2192blend'),(180,'blend\\u2192raise2')]:\\n    r  = cam_radius(b)\\n    vb = (cam_radius(b) - cam_radius(b - eps)) / eps\\n    va = (cam_radius(b + eps) - cam_radius(b)) / eps\\n    vj = abs(va - vb)\\n    sym = \\\"OK\\\" if vj < 0.1 else \\\"WARN\\\"\\n    print(f\\\"  [{sym}] {b:3d}\\u00b0 R={r:.4f}mm  vel_jump={vj:.4f}  [{zone}]\\\")\\n\\nprint()\\nprint(\\\"Profile (key angles, one 180\\u00b0 cycle):\\\")\\nfor ang, lbl in [(0,'RAISE start'),(25,'raise mid'),(50,'HOLD peak'),\\n                  (80,'STITCH start'),(110,'stitch mid'),(140,'LOWER start'),\\n                  (155,'lower mid'),(170,'BLEND start'),(175,'blend peak'),\\n                  (179,'blend end'),(180,'next cycle start')]:\\n    r = cam_radius(ang)\\n    print(f\\\"  {ang:3d}\\u00b0  lift={r-R_BASE:.3f}mm  [{lbl}]\\\")\\n\\nprint()\\nprint(\\\"V5 fixes applied:\\\")\\nprint(\\\"  A: blend 0.1*sin^2 (peak=0.1mm, was 0.2mm)\\\")\\nprint(\\\"  B: pin offset side*(half+2.0) (centered on insert body)\\\")\\nprint(\\\"  C: set screw wall 1.5mm (was 1.0mm, crack risk)\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# BUILD\\n# =============================================================================\\nprint(\\\"\\\\nBuilding geometry...\\\")\\npts = []\\nfor i in range(POINTS):\\n    a = 2.0 * math.pi * i / POINTS\\n    r = cam_radius(math.degrees(a))\\n    pts.append(FreeCAD.Vector(r * math.cos(a), r * math.sin(a), 0.0))\\npts.append(pts[0])\\n\\nface    = Part.Face(Part.makePolygon(pts))\\nprofile = face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H))\\n\\nouter = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT, CAM_H)\\ncam   = outer.cut(profile)\\n\\nshft = Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_R, CAM_H + 2)\\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\\ncam  = cam.cut(shft)\\n\\n# Registration groove\\nt_out = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE + TRACK_D, TRACK_W)\\nt_in  = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE - INSERT_H) / 2.0\\n    pin_r = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\\n\\n    # Main slot\\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H)\\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, z0))\\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n    body = body.cut(slot)\\n\\n    # FIX B: guide pins at side*(half+2.0) \\u2014 centered on insert body\\n    for z in [z0 + 4.0, z0 + INSERT_H - =============================================================================\ndef cam_radius(theta_deg):\n    t = theta_deg % 180.0\n    if t < 50.0:\n        x = t / 50.0\n        return R_BASE + elif t < 170.0:\n        x = (t - 140.0) / 30.0\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT - STITCH_DROP) * (math.cos(math.pi * x / 2.0))**2\n    else:\n        x = (t - 170.0) / 10.0\n        return R_BASE + 0.1 * (math.sin(math.pi * x))**2\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD GEOMETRY\n# =============================================================================\nprint(\"\\nBuilding cam geometry...\")\n\npts = []\nfor i in range(POINTS):\n    a = 2.0 * math.pi * i / POINTS\n    r = cam_radius(math.degrees(a))\n    pts.append(FreeCAD.Vector(r * math.cos(a), r * math.sin(a), 0.0))\npts.append(pts[0])\n\nface    = Part.Face(Part.makePolygon(pts))\nprofile = face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H))\nouter   = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT, CAM_H)\ncam     = outer.cut(profile)\n\nshft = Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_R, CAM_H + 2)\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\ncam = cam.cut(shft)\n\nt_out = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE + TRACK_D, TRACK_W)\nt_in  = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE - 0.1, TRACK_W + 2)\nt_out.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0))\nt_in.translate( FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H / 2.0 - TRACK_W / 2.0 - 1))\ncam = cam.cut(t_out.cut(t_in))\n\ndef insert_pocket(center_ang_deg, body):\n    half  = INSERT_CHORD / 2.0\n    z0    = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0\n    pin_r = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\n\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H)\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, z0))\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\n    body = body.cut(slot)\n\n    for z in [z0 + 4.0, z0 + INSERT_H - 4.0]:\n        for side in [-1, 1]:\n            ph = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, PIN_DEPTH + 2)\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\n            ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(side * (half + 2.0), pin_r, z))\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\n            body = body.cut(ph)\n\n    ss_len = CAM_WALL - 1.5\n    for chord_off in [-half * 0.5, half * 0.5]:\n        ss = Part.makeCylinder(1.6, ss_len)\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\n        ss.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(chord_off, CAM_R_OUT - ss_len, CAM_H / 2.0))\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\n        body = body.cut(ss)\n\n    return body\n\ncam = insert_pocket(STITCH1_ANG, cam)\ncam = insert_pocket(STITCH2_ANG, cam)\n\nfor i in range(4):\n    ang = math.radians(i * 90.0 + 45.0)\n    bx, by = BOLT_R * math.cos(ang), BOLT_R * math.sin(ang)\n    bh = Part.makeCylinder(5.5 / 2.0, CAM_H + 2)\n    bh.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, -1))\n    cam = cam.cut(bh)\n    cs = Part.makeCylinder(4.5, 5.0)\n    cs.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, CAM_H - 5.0))\n    cam = cam.cut(cs)\n\nfor i in range(APIN_N):\n    ang = math.radians(i * 120.0 + 30.0)\n    px, py = APIN_R * math.cos(ang), APIN_R * math.sin(ang)\n    ph = Part.makeCylinder(APIN_D / 2.0 + 0.05, APIN_DEPTH)\n    ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(px, py, 0))\n    cam = cam.cut(ph)\n\nks = Part.makeBox(KEY_W + 0.15, KEY_DEPTH + 0.1, KEY_W * 5)\nks.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-(KEY_W + 0.15) / 2.0,\n                              R_BASE - KEY_DEPTH - 0.1,\n                              CAM_H / 2.0 - KEY_W * 2.5))\ncam = cam.cut(ks)\n\nsg = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT - 0.5, 1.5)\nsi = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT - 3.0, 2.5)\nsg.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 1.0))\nsi.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, 0.5))\ncam = cam.cut(sg.cut(si))\n\nobj = doc.addObject(\"Part::Feature\", \"CSM_CamRing_V5\")\nobj.Shape = cam\nobj.ViewObject.ShapeColor = (0.10, 0.28, 0.52)\ndoc.recompute()\nprint(\"  Geometry complete \u2705\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# EXPORT (Auto-Export)\\\")\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 58)\\nprint(f\\\"  Output folder: {BASE}\\\")\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# ALL PARAMETERS\\n# 10.0\\nKEY_W     = 3.0\\nKEY_DEPTH = 1.5\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# CAM PROFILE FUNCTION \\u2014 V5 Final\\n# =============================================================================\\ndef cam_radius(theta_deg):\\n    t = theta_deg % 180.0\\n    if t < 50.0:\\n        x = t / 50.0\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT * (1.0 - math.cos(math.pi * x)) / 2.0\\n    elif t < 80.0:\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT\\n    elif t < 140.0:\\n        x = (t - 80.0) / 60.0\\n        return R_BASE + LIFT - STITCH_DROP * x\\n    elif t < 170.0:\\n        x = (t - 140.0) / 30.0\\n        return R_BASE + (LIFT - STITCH_DROP) * (math.cos(math.pi * x / 2.0))**2\\n    else:\\n        x = (t - 170.0) / 10.0\\n        return face.extrude(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, CAM_H))\\nouter   = Part.makeCylinder(CAM_R_OUT, CAM_H)\\ncam     = outer.cut(profile)\\n\\nshft = Part.makeCylinder(SHAFT_R, CAM_H + 2)\\nshft.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(0, 0, -1))\\ncam = cam.cut(shft)\\n\\nt_out = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE + TRACK_D, TRACK_W)\\nt_in  = Part.makeCylinder(R_BASE - 0.1, TRACK_W + insert_pocket(center_ang_deg, body):\\n    half  = INSERT_CHORD / 2.0\\n    z0    = (CAM_H - INSERT_H) / 2.0\\n    pin_r = INSERT_R_IN + STITCH_Z / 2.0\\n\\n    slot = Part.makeBox(SLOT_W, STITCH_Z, INSERT_H)\\n    slot.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(-SLOT_W / 2.0, INSERT_R_IN, z0))\\n    slot.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n    body = body.cut(slot)\\n\\n    for z in [z0 + 4.0, z0 + INSERT_H - 4.0]:\\n        for side in [-1, 1]:\\n            ph = Part.makeCylinder(PIN_HOLE_D / 2.0, PIN_DEPTH + 2)\\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\\n            ph.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(side * (half + 2.0), pin_r, z))\\n            ph.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n            body = body.cut(ph)\\n\\n    ss_len = CAM_WALL - 1.5\\n    for chord_off in [-half * 0.5, half * 0.5]:\\n        ss = Part.makeCylinder(1.6, ss_len)\\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,1,0), 90)\\n        ss.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(chord_off, CAM_R_OUT - ss_len, CAM_H / 2.0))\\n        ss.rotate(FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,0), FreeCAD.Vector(0,0,1), center_ang_deg)\\n        body = body.cut(ss)\\n\\n    return body\\n\\ncam = insert_pocket(STITCH1_ANG, cam)\\ncam = insert_pocket(STITCH2_ANG, cam)\\n\\nfor i in range(4):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 90.0 + 45.0)\\n    bx, Part.makeCylinder(4.5, 5.0)\\n    cs.translate(FreeCAD.Vector(bx, by, CAM_H - 5.0))\\n    cam = cam.cut(cs)\\n\\nfor i in range(APIN_N):\\n    ang = math.radians(i * 120.0 + 30.0)\\n    px, py =
